patippuri
tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum vaḷamum valimiyum cērkkak
kūṭiya paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷiyellām tēṭiyeṭuttut tamiḻ kūṟum ulakiṟku vaḻaṅkum tolinōkkup pārviyuṭaṉ  tamiḻmaṇ patippakam toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. ataṉ vāyilāka moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ nūṟṟāṇṭu niṉivāka avar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ aṉittiyum cērttu avaruṭiya maṟivukkup piṟaku 2000-ttilum, palvēṟu itaḻkaḷilum, malarkaḷilum veḷivantu, nūl vaṭivam peṟāta pāvāṇariṉ ariya kaṭṭurikaḷi ellām tokuttu 2001- lum orucēra veḷiyiṭṭu ulakeṅkum uḷḷa tamiḻarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkiṉōm.  pāvāṇar vaḻi nili aṟiñarkaḷāṉa mutumuṉivar 
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum, maruttuvar ku.pūṅkāvaṉam avarkaḷum ivvariya kaṭṭurip putiyalkaḷ nūl vaṭivam peṟuvataṟku mikavum uṟutuṇiyāka iruntaṉar. ipperumakkaḷi naṉṟiyuṇarvuṭaṉ niṉivukūrkiṟēṉ.
collārāyccit tuṟiyil tēvanēyar oppaṟṟa taṉit
tiṟamiyuṭivar eṉṟu maṟimaliyaṭikaḷum, nāvalan tīvukku  nantamiḻē tāyeṉṟu pāvēntarum, tamiḻukkum tamiḻ
nāṭṭukkum naṟṟoṇṭu āṟṟiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pēraṟiñar aṇṇā avarkaḷum, veṭṭa veṭṭak kiṭikkum taṅkac
curaṅkam pōṉṟavar eṉṟu tamiḻiṟiñar irācamāṇikkaṉārum, tamiḻakam moḻittuṟiyilē pāvāṇar pōṉṟa oru aṟiñari iṉṉum peṟṟut taravilli eṉṟu paṉmoḻip pulavar
appātturiyarum, kuṟimatiyar tēkkivitta kariyiruḷi nīkka vanta maṟimaliyār vaḻivanta niṟimaliyār pāvāṇar eṉṟu mēṉāḷ pēravittalivar tamiḻkuṭimakaṉ avarkaḷum, tamiḻar yār? etirikaḷ yār? eṉṟu āyntu aṟintu kāṭṭiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pērāciriyar iḷavaracu avarkaḷum,  ōrāyiram āṇṭukaḷ ōyntu kiṭantapiṉ vārātu pōla vanta māmaṇi pāvāṇar eṉṟu mutumuṉivar iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷa  perumikaḷukkuriya pēraṟiñariṉ nūlkaḷi mīḷpatippaka veḷiyiṭuvatil perumi koḷkiṟēṉ.
pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ attaṉiyum tamiḻmoḻikku ēṟṟam urippaṉa. tamiḻi āriya iruḷiṉiṉṟu mīṭṭuk kāppaṉa. vīḻntu
paṭṭa tamiḻaṉukku viḻippūṭṭa vallaṉa. āyiramāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka aṭimippaṭṭukkiṭakkum tamiḻmoḻiyi mīṭkavallatu eṉṟu  pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār avarkaḷiṉ kūṟṟi ik kaḷañciya veḷiyīṭṭil pativu ceyvatu ematu kaṭamiyākum.
pāvāṇarit tūkkip piṭittāltāṉ tamiḻiṉam urup
paṭamuṭiyum - uyaramuṭiyum. pāvāṇar koḷkikaḷ tamiḻar uḷḷamellām nilittu niṟpataṟkum, pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ tamiḻar illamelām iṭam peṟuvataṟkum muṉ kuṟippiṭṭa 2000 - 2001 kālakkaṭṭattil veḷiyiṭappaṭṭa pāvāṇariṉ aṟivuk karuvūlaṅkaḷiyellām oṭṭu mottamāka cērttu orē vīccil pāvāṇar tamiḻk kaḷañciyam eṉum talippil  em aṟakkaṭṭaḷi veḷiyiṭukiṟatu.
maṟikkappeṟṟa māperum varalāṟṟiyum, citikkap peṟṟa oppuyarvuyaṟṟa moḻiyiyum koṇṭa tamiḻiṉattiṉ muṉṉēṟṟam karuti ivvaruntamiḻ putiyalkaḷi tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakiṟku  vaḻaṅka muṉ vantuḷḷōm. tamiḻ moḻiyi mūccākavum, pēccākavum, ceyalākavum koṇṭu orunāḷiṉ muḻuppoḻutum tamiḻākavē vāḻnta cempulaccemmal,  taṉittamiḻk katiravaṉ moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar nūlkaḷi vāṅkip payaṉ koḷvīr.
iḷamiyilēyē potuttoṇṭilum, taṉittamiḻ iyakkat toṇṭilum nāṉ īṭupāṭu koḷvataṟkuk kāraṇamāka iruntavarkaḷ perumpulavar nakkīraṉ avarkaḷum, avartam iḷaval pulavar cittiravēlaṉ avarkaḷum āvar. ivarkaḷi ivveḷiyiṭṭiṉ vāyilāka naṉṟiyuṇarvōṭu niṉivu kūṟukiṟēṉ.  tanti periyāriṉ taṉmatippu iyakkak koḷki
kaḷālum, moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ taṉittamiḻ iyakkak koḷkikaḷālum īrkkappaṭṭavaṉ. attaku piṉpulattōṭu patippuppaṇiyil eṉ kālaṭic cuvaṭukaḷip patittu varukiṟēṉ.
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ,
patippāḷar.

iyaṉilip paṭalam
(3) iṭiccol
perumpālum tamittuvarātu, peyarukkum viṉikkum uṟuppāka avaṟṟiṉiṭattu varum coṟkaḷ iṭiccollām. iṭi iṭam. ciṟupāṉmi tamittum varuvatāl tamittu varuvaṉa, colliṭattu varuvaṉa eṉa iṭiccoṟkaḷ irupāṟpaṭum. tamittu varuvaṉavum, iyaṟkiyiṭic collum ceyaṟki yiṭiccollum āka irutiṟappaṭum. iṭiccollākavē tōṉṟiyavi iyaṟki; peyarum viṉiyum palvēṟu poru ḷuṇartti iṭiccollākat tirintavi ceyaṟki.
1. tamittu varuvaṉa
Rṭṭiṭiccoṟkaḷv-L:
	antā, atō, aṉṉā
	intā, itō, iṉṉā
	uva, utu
ā, a, ī, i, ū, u eṉpaṉa anta, inta, unta eṉṟu poruḷ tarum cuṭṭuk kuṟippup peyareccaṅkaḷāyiṉum (Demonstrative 
Adjectives) tamittu vaḻaṅkāmiyiṉ, iṭiccollākak koḷḷap paṭum.

iṇippuc coṟkaḷ (Conjunctions)
1. aṭukkiṇippuc coṟkaḷ (Cumulative Conjunctions)
e-ṭu: 	eṉa, eṉavum, eṉā, eṉṟu, eṉṟum, eṉṟā. ivi coṟkaḷiyum coṟṟoṭarkaḷiyum iṇikkum.
2. maṟuppiṇippuc coṟkaḷ (Adversative Conjunctions)
e-ṭu: 	āṉāl, āṉālum, āyiṉ, āyiṉum, iruntālum, 	iruntapōtilum, iruntāṟkūṭa, eṉiṉum, eṉ ṟālum. ivi coṟṟoṭarkaḷiyē iṇikkum.
3. maṟunili yiṇippuc coṟkaḷ (Alternative Conjunctions)
e-ṭu: allatu, eṉiṉum, eṉṟālum, eṉṟō.
ivaṟṟuḷ, allatu colliyum coṟṟoṭariyum ïṇikkum;bcālliÆṇippiṉ,xvvōrïrubcālÈṭiyumxvvoruKṟitrum;Vṉiyabcāṟkaḷikṭṭumïṇikkum.4. uyttuṇar viṇippuc coṟkaḷ (Illative Conjunctions)
e-ṭu:	ākiyāl, ākiyiṉāl, ātalāl, ākavē, eṉavē. ivi coṟṟoṭarkaḷi iṇippaṉa.
taṉip poruḷiṭic coṟkaḷ 
eṉa
itu karaṇiyam (kāraṇam), poruṭṭu, peyarīṭu, iṇippu, olikkuṟippu, virivuk kuṟippu, vaṇṇakkuṟippu, kālakkuṟippu mutaliya poruḷkaḷil varum.
itu eṉ eṉṉum viṉiyiṉ akaravīṟṟu eccamākum.
veḷḷeṉa vā-kālakkuṟippu
veḷḷeṉa = kiḻakkuveḷukkumpōtu, 					viṭikāliyil,kuṟitjnērattiṟFmuṉpē.
eṉṟu
itu peyarīṭu, iṇippu, olikkuṟippu, virivuk kuṟippu, vaṇṇakkuṟippu mutaliya poruḷkaḷil varum.
itu eṉ eṉṉum viṉiyiṉ iṟantakāla eccamākum.
	ulaka vaḻakkil, nērkūṟṟi (Direct Speech) muṭikkuñ col lōṭu iṇippatu iḵtoṉṟē.
e-ṭu: avaṉ nāḷi varuvāṉ eṉṟu coṉṉāṉ.
muṭikkuñcol eṉṟāṉ eṉṉum viṉimuṟṟāyiṉ, iṇippuc col varātu.
e-ṭu: avaṉ nāḷi varuvēṉ eṉṟāṉ.
aṭi aṭi eṉṟu aṭittāṉ, tiṉ tiṉ eṉṟu tiṉṟāṉ eṉpavaṟṟil, eṉṟu eṉṉum col kūṟṟik kuṟiyātu viṉi mikutiyi allatu kaṭumiyi uṇarttum.
maṉṟa eṉpatu tēṟṟamum, tañcam eṉpatu eḷimiyum, ellē eṉpatu irakkamum uṇarttum.
ellē yilakkam. (tol.iṭi. 21) eṉṉum pāṭam tavaṟāṉatākum. el eṉpatu oḷiyiyum pakaliyum katiravaṉiyum kuṟikkum peyarccollātalāl, iṭiccol lākātu. ētilēṉ araṅkaṟ kellē  eṉṟu, nālāyirat teyvap paṉuvalil (tirumāli. 26) ellē eṉpatu  iraṅkaṟ poruḷil vantiruttal kāṇka.
tālāṭ ṭiṭiccoṟkaḷ
e-ṭu: lālā, rōrō.
ivi ilakkiya naṭiyil tālā, ōrō eṉṟeḻutap peṟum. puḷḷōppa liṭiccol: ālōlam.
pitaṟṟaṟkuṟip piṭiccoṟkaḷ

2. colliṭattu varuvaṉa
colliṭattu varum iṭiccoṟkaḷ, (1) muṉṉoṭṭukaḷ, (2) variciyiṭiccoṟkaḷ, (3) iṇippuccoṟkaḷ, (4) palporu ḷiṭic coṟkaḷ, (5) īṟukaḷ, (6) iṭinilikaḷ, (7) cāriyikaḷ, (8) vēṟṟumi yurupukaḷ, eṉa eṇvakippaṭum.
(1) muṉṉoṭṭukaḷ (Prefixes)
e-ṭu: 
al - a: avalam.
al: aḵṟiṇi, alvaḻi, aṉmoḻi.
miku - mī: mīkkūṟṟu, mīccelavu, mīntōl.
nal - na: nakkīraṉ, nacceḷḷi, nattattaṉ, nappacali.
akavalippaṭuttutal, iṭiccerukal, uṭkōḷ, uṭaṉ piṟappu, uḻitarutal, uḻiccelvāṉ, ūṭuruval, kaṭittēṟal, kīḻppaṭital, talikkūṭal, naṭuttīrppu, piṟpōkku, puṟaṅkūṟṟu, maṟumuḷ pāytal, muṉṉēṟṟam, mēṟpārvi, vaḻimoḻital, veḷiyiṭutal eṉṉum coṟkaḷiṉ mutaliluḷḷa akam iṭi, uḷ, uṭaṉ, uḻi, uḻi, ūṭu, kaṭi, kīḻ, tali, naṭu, piṉ, puṟam, maṟu, muṉ, mēl, vaḻi, veḷi eṉṉum muṉṉoṭṭukaḷum, iṭic collāka āḷappeṟṟa piṟacoṟkaḷē. ivaṟṟiṉ nēr āṅkila allatu āriyaccoṟkaḷ perumpālum tūya muṉṉoṭṭukaḷā yiruttal kāṇka. āriyaccoṟkaḷ tiricoṟkaḷātaliṉ, tōṟṟam maṟintuḷḷaṉa; tamiḻccoṟkaḷ ellām iyaṟcoṟkaḷātaliṉ, tōṟṟanteḷivāyum tamittu vaḻaṅkuvaṉavāyu muḷḷaṉa. ituvē ivitammuḷ vēṟṟumi.

ām - oṉṟām, mutalām, nūṟām.

paṭarkkippeya rīṟukaḷ
āṇpālīṟukaḷ:
āḷ eṉṉum uyartiṇiccol, āḷkiṟavaṉ eṉṉum karuttil āṇpāliyum, āḷappaṭukiṟavaḷ eṉṉum karuttil peṇpāliyum uṇarttum.
āṇpāli yuṇarttum iṭaṅkaḷ:
1. āṭavaṉ nallā ḷilāta kuṭi	(kuṟaḷ. 1030)
2. tiṟavōṉ. āḷallāṉ celvak kuṭiyuṭ piṟattalum     	(tirikaṭu. 7)
3. pōrmaṟavaṉ piṇampiṟaṅka āḷeṟintu 	(pu.ve. 2:7)
4. kālāḷ. āḷveḷḷam pōkavum 	(pu.ve. 7:13)
5. kaṇavaṉ. āḷillā maṅkik kaḻaku         	(vākkuṇṭām,3)
āḷaṉ=	1. āḷpavaṉ
	2. kaṇavaṉ. āḷaṉ illāta tukkam aḻutālum tīrātu.
peṇṭāḷutal eṉṉum vaḻakkāṟu, maṉivi kaṇavaṉāl āḷap peṟutali uṇarttum.
āḷ eṉṉuñ col pāṟpotumi nīṅki āṭavaṉik kuṟittaṟku, āṇ eṉṟu tirintatu. āṇ eṉṉum collē, āṉ eṉṟu tirintu āṇpālīṟāṉatākat terikiṉṟatu. ṇakaram vaṭatiraviṭa moḻikaḷiṟ potuvāka ṉakaramākat tirivatāl, āṇ eṉṉum col aṅku āṉ eṉṟutāṉ irukkum. āṉīṟu piṉṉar aṉ eṉṟu kuṟukiṟṟu. e-ṭu: avaṉ, celvaṉ.
aṉṉīṟu piṉpu al eṉat tirintatu. ṉakara meyyīṟu lakara meyyīṟākat tirital iyalpē.
v-L: 	âw«-âw‹-âwš, brŒbt‹-brŒt‹-brŒtš, Mš-M‹ (3M« nt.c.), mēl-mēṉ.
aṇṇal, iḷaval, cemmal, vaḷḷal mutaliya peyarkaḷiṉ allīṟu āṇpālīṟē. kuricil eṉpatu kurucal eṉpataṉ tiripā yirukkalām. o.nō: parical-paricil. 
āṉīṟu ōṉ eṉṟum tiriyum e-ṭu: muṉṉāṉ-muṉṉōṉ.
māntaṉiyum piḷḷiyiyum kuṟikkum maka eṉṉum col aṉṉīṟupeṟṟu makaṉ eṉṟākum. atuvum ōr āṇpālīṟām.
e-ṭu: perumakaṉ, tirumakaṉ, turimakaṉ. makaṉ eṉṉum īṟu māṉ eṉṟu maruvum.
e-ṭu: 	perumakaṉ - perumāṉ, tirumakaṉ - tirumāṉ, maru makaṉ - marumāṉ.
māṉ īṟu maṉ eṉṟu kuṟukum.
e-ṭu: vaṭamaṉ.
aṉṉīṟu peṟṟa appaṉ, aiyaṉ, aṇṇaṉ eṉṉum muṟip peyarkaḷum, arumi kuṟitta āṇpālīṟāka vaḻaṅkum.
e-ṭu: kaṇṇappaṉ, poṉṉiyaṉ, karuppaṇṇaṉ.
pāl tōṉṟāta mutaṟkālattil ikaravīṟu āṇpāl, peṇpāl, oṉṟaṉpāl ākiya orumippāl mūṉṟiyum uṇartti vantatu. av vaḻakku iṉṟumuḷatu.
e-ṭu: 	toḻilāḷi, viṟakuveṭṭi - āṇpāl
      	kiḻavi, kayaṟkaṇṇi - peṇpāl
       	maṇveṭṭi, kāṭikkaṇṇi - oṉṟaṉpāl
aṉṉīṟu peṟṟa āḷaṉ eṉṉum collum, ikaravīṟu peṟṟa āḷi eṉṉum collum, āṇ pālīṟu pōṉṟum vaḻaṅkum.
e-ṭu:	vēḷāḷaṉ, tāḷāḷaṉ.
      	mutalāḷi, maliyāḷi.
iṉi, uṭimiyum urimiyum uṇarttum kāraṉ eṉṉum collum āṇpālīṟām.
e-ṭu: vīṭṭukkāraṉ, vaṇṭikkāraṉ.
tiyaṟkāraṉ, vēlikkāraṉ, kūlikkāraṉ eṉṉum coṟ kaḷum, tiyal vēlikkum vēli ceytaṟkum kūlit toḻi lukkum uriyavaṉ allatu at toḻilkaḷi uṭiyavaṉ eṉṟē poruḷ paṭum.
kaṭumi = mikuti, valimi. kaṭu - kaṭi - kari - kāram = kaṭumi, mikuti, valimi, atikāram, urimi. kāram - kāraṉ = urimiyāḷaṉ, uṭiyavaṉ.
vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa kru eṉṉum viṉiyaṭiyākap piṟantu, ceypavaṉik kuṟikkum kāra eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu kāraṉ eṉṉum āṇpālīṟu tirintatāka vaṭavar kūṟuvatu poruntātu. āṭṭukkāraṉ, kaṭikāraṉ, kappaṟkāraṉ, kāyccaṟkāraṉ, kuṭikāraṉ, kuruvikkāraṉ, kōḻikkāraṉ, contakkāraṉ, taṭṭukkāraṉ, tōṭṭakkāraṉ, nilattukkāraṉ, paṭṭakkāraṉ, paṇakkāraṉ, pāḷiyakkāraṉ, piḷḷi kuṭṭikkāraṉ, puṉceyk kāraṉ, puḷḷikkāraṉ, poṟumikkāraṉ, poṟāmikkāraṉ, māṭṭukkāraṉ, muṭṭikkāraṉ mutaliya eṇ ṇiṟanta peyarkaṭku, urimip poruḷaṉṟic ceykipporuḷ ciṟitum porun tāmi kāṇka.
peṇpālīṟukaḷ
āḷ eṉṉum col, āṭavaṉukku āṭpaṭukiṟavaḷ eṉṉum karuttil peṉpālīṟāyiṟṟu. cēranāṭṭut tamiḻiṉ tiripākiya maliyāḷattil, āḷ eṉṉuñ col annāṭṭu vaḻakkiṟkēṟpa ōkāra mutaṟcollāyt tirintu, maṉiviyik kuṟikkiṉṟatu. atu teluṅkil ālu eṉṟu tirintu peṇṇik kuṟikkiṉṟatu, kūmoḻiyilum iṅṅaṉamē ātalāl, paṇṭit tamiḻilum iv vaḻakku iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. iṉṟum, maṉiyāḷ eṉṉum muṟip peyarum vantāḷ eṉṉum viṉiyālaṇiyum peyarum, maṉiyōḷ, vantōḷ eṉa maliyāḷatti yottut tamiḻilum tirital kāṇka.
āḷ īṟu aḷ eṉṟu kuṟukum.
e-ṭu: avaḷ, makaḷ.
aḷḷīṟu peṟṟa makaḷ eṉṉum peyarum māḷ eṉṟu maruvip peṇpālīṟām.
e-ṭu:	vēṇmakaḷ - vēṇmāḷ, perumakaḷ - perumāḷ.
	(perumāl - perumāḷ = viṭṭuṇu)
cila aṉṉīṟṟu āṇpāṟ peyarkaḷ peṇpālil ai yīṟākat tiriyum.
e-ṭu: āciriyaṉ - āciriyi, aiyaṉ-aiyi, civaṉ - civi, paṇṭitaṉ - paṇṭiti, parattaṉ - paratti, paraṉ - pari, valavaṉ -valavi. paṇṭiyorumi yīṟākiya ikaravīṟum peṇpālīṟātal muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭatu.
e-ṭu: 	celvaṉ-celvi, pulavaṉ-pulatti.
       	kaḷḷaṉ-kaḷḷi, kiḻavaṉ-kiḻatti.
cila āṇpāl aṉṉīṟu peṇpālil iṉi eṉṟu tiriyum.
e-ṭu: pāṇaṉ-pāṇiṉi-pāṭiṉi.
cila āṇpāl aṉaṉ īṟu peṇpālil aṉi eṉṟu tiriyum.
e-ṭu: pārppaṉaṉ-pārppaṉi.
aṉṉiyik kuṟikkum ammi, acci eṉṉum peyarkaḷ arumipaṟṟip peṇpālīṟām.
e-ṭu: kaṇṇammi, taṅkicci.
atti acci eṉṉum aṉṉi peyarkaḷ, kulamum toḻilum paṟṟiya  poṇpālīṟāy vaḻaṅkum.
e-ṭu: maṟatti, maruttuvacci.
atti, acci eṉpaṉa itti, icci eṉṟun tiriyum.
e-ṭu: vēṭṭuvitti, kaṭṭuvicci.
cila peṇpāṟ peyarkaḷ, āṇpāṟkuriya maṟamum āṇmiyu muṇartta āṇpālīṟu koḷḷum.
e-ṭu: ammi-ammaṉ, pēṭu-pēṭaṉ.
ammi eṉṉuñ colli yottu, akki eṉṉum collum akkaṉ eṉṟu tiriyum.
āṇmi koṇṭa peṇṇip pēṭaṉ eṉpatu pōl peṇmi(peṇṭaṉmi) koṇṭa āṇip pēṭi eṉpatu marapu. iv viraṇṭiṟkum potuvāṉatu pēṭu eṉṉuñ col. āṇum peṇṇum allātatu allatu kalantatu ali. ip peyarkaḷ viṉi koḷḷumpōtu, pēṭaṉ vantāṉ, pēṭi vantāḷ, pēṭu vantatu, ali vantatu eṉa āṭṭaṉmiyum collīṟumpaṟṟi varum. itu ilakkaṇa marapu.
ulaka vaḻakkil, pēṭiyip peṭṭiyaṉ eṉṟum, āṇmi yaṟṟavaṉip peṇṇiyaṉ allatu aṇṇakaṉ eṉṟum, uruvam paṟṟip pēṭi vantāṉ, peṇṇiyaṉ vantāṉ, aṇṇakaṉ vantāṉ eṉṟum, kūṟuvatē marapām. aliyāyiṉ, āṇaliyi vantāṉ eṉṟum, peṇṇaliyi vantāḷ eṉṟum, uruvattiṟ kēṟpak kūṟuvar.
āḷaṉ eṉṉum āṇpālīṟu, peṇpālil āṭṭi eṉṟu tiriyum. āḷ + ti = āṭṭi. ti eṉpatu atti eṉpataṉ kuṟukkam.
e-ṭu: 	kaṇṇāḷaṉ - kaṇṇāṭṭi, tiruvāḷaṉ - tiruvāṭṭi, veḷḷāḷaṉ - veḷḷāṭṭi.
kāraṉ eṉṉum āṇpālīṟu, peṉpāliṟ kāri eṉṟu tiriyum.
e-ṭu:	keṭṭikkāraṉ - keṭṭikkāri, paṇakkāraṉ - paṇakkāri.
māṉīṟṟu āṇpāṟ peyarkaṭ kotta peṇpāṟ peyarkaḷ, māṭṭi eṉṉum īṟu koḷḷum. makaḷ - māḷ + ti = māṭṭi.
e-ṭu: tirumāṉ - tirumāṭṭi, perumāṉ - perumāṭṭi.
tiru eṉṉum col srī eṉṟum, tirumāṉ eṉṉum col srīmat eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum. srīmat eṉpataṉ peṇpāl srīmatī-tirumati
cila peṇpāṟ peyarkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyilum teṉmoḻi yilum vevvēṟu vakiyil amintu, oṉṟupōl tōṉṟum.
e-ṭu: 	pati-patnī (va.) = maṉivi.
	pattaṉ-pattiṉi(te.) = kaṇavaṉiṭattiṟ pattipūṇ ṭavaḷ, kaṟpuṭi maṉivi.
cila peṇpāṟ peyarkaḷ ikara vīṟṟuṭaṉ icci yīṟuṅ koḷḷum.
e-ṭu:	kuruvikkāraṉ - kuruvikkāricci, veḷḷikkāraṉ - veḷḷikkāricci.
māṟōkkam eṉṉum koṟki nāṭṭār, paṇṭi nāḷil ciṟumiyip peṇmakaṉ eṉṟaṉar;  iṉṟu vaṭārkkāṭṭār peṭṭip pacaṉ eṉpar. piyaṉ-payaṉ-pacaṉ.
palarpālīṟukaḷ
ārtal = poruntutal, kūṭutal, niṟital.
ār = poruttu, niṟivu.
palar kūṭutal eṉṉum karuttil ār eṉṉuñ collē palarpālīṟāyiṟṟu.
e-ṭu: taṭṭār, pollār.
ārīṟu ar eṉak kuṟukum.
e-ṭu: avar, palar, kollar.
ārīṟu ōr eṉṟum tiriyum.
e-ṭu: periyār - periyōr, muṉṉār - muṉṉōr.
ār īṟu peṟṟa makār eṉṉum peyarum mār eṉa maruvip palar pālīṟām. maka-makār-mār.
e-ṭu: aṇṇaṉmār, tēvimār.
ceyyum eṉṉum etirkāla viṉimuṟṟōṭu ārīṟu cērum pōtu, oru mār tōṉṟum. atu viṉiyīṟu. ceyyum + ār =  ceyyumār - ceymār - ceyvār.
uyartiṇikku uriyataṉṟeṉṟu vilakkappaṭṭa kaḷ īṟu,  ciṟupāṉmi at tiṇikkum varum.
e-ṭu:	makkaḷ, kōkkaḷ, kurukkaḷ, tirukkaḷ, nāṅkaḷ, nīṅkaḷ, avarkaḷ, tāṅkaḷ, āḷkaḷ, āṇkaḷ, peṇkaḷ, piyaṉkaḷ. makkaḷ eṉṉum peyar tol kāppiyattilum uḷḷatu.
uyartiṇi yeṉmaṉār makkaṭ cuṭṭē.	(484)
makkaḷ tāmē āṟṟi vuyirē.	(1532)
maka + kaḷ = makkaḷ. itu marūup puṇarcci.
o.nō: aka + kaḷippu = akkaḷippu.
makkaḷi iḻintōr, ottōr, uyarntōr eṉa muttiṟattā rāka vakuttu, avari muṟiyē nī, nīr, nīṅkaḷ; avaṉ, (avaḷ), avar, avarkaḷ eṉṉum coṟkaḷāṟ kuṟippatu tūya ulakavaḻakkam. paṉmiyilakkaṇamellām ceyyuḷ naṭikkē eḻutappeṟṟati ṉāl iv vaḻakku atil iṭampeṟavilli.
aṭikaḷ eṉpatu tiruvaṭi nōkkik kaṭavuḷik kuṟikkum. atu tuṟaviṉāl teyvattaṉmi peṟṟa periyōrik kuṟikkum pōtu irupāṟpotuvām.
e-ṭu: 	iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ, kavuntiyaṭikaḷ.
	ārīṟum kaḷḷīṟum uyarvupaṟṟi orumikkum varum.
e-ṭu: 	makaṉār, nakkīraṉār, aṭikaḷār, vaḷḷalār,
	nīṅkaḷ, avarkaḷ, tāṅkaḷ, kurukkaḷ.
ceyyuḷnaṭi, urinaṭi eṉa ilakkiya naṭi iruvakip paṭum. ceyyuḷnaṭikku vilakkappaṭṭa ulakavaḻakku urinaṭikku varum eṉa aṟika. ulakavaḻakkeṉpatu uyarntōr vaḻakkēyaṉṟi iḻintōr vaḻakkaṉṟu.
oṉṟaṉpālīṟukaḷ
tiṇipāl tōṉṟāta mutupaṇṭik kālattil, oṉṟu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum oṉ eṉṉuñ col orumiyuṇarttiṟṟu. atu puṇarcciyil tokku ṉ aḷavāyum niṟkum.
e-ṭu: nāṉ, nīṉ, tāṉ.
oṉṉīṟu aṉṉīṟākavum tiriyum. aṉṉīṟu āṉīṟām.
e-ṭu:	neṭuṅkaḻuttaṉ (oṭṭakam), coṟiyaṉ (tavaḷi), 		kaṭuvaṉ; uḷḷāṉ, karippāṉ, kattarippāṉ.
āṇpāl aṉṉīṟupōl, paṇṭi yorumi aṉṉīṟum al  eṉat tiriyum.
e-ṭu: uḷḷāṉ - uḷḷal, neṭuṅkaḻuttaṉ - neṭuṅkaḻuttal.   
cuṭṭeḻuttukaḷum mutaṟkālattil īṟāyiruntu orumi yuṇarttiṉatākat terikiṉṟatu. avaṟṟuḷ ikaramē iṉṟu vaḻak kiluḷḷatu. i = itu.
e-ṭu: kutirivāli, paṉiyēṟi (mīṉ).
alli, palli, puli (pulli), kiḷi (kiḷḷi) mutaliyaṉavum, kuṉṉi, naṉṉi mutaliyaṉavum, ikaravīṟṟup peyarkaḷē.
aimpālīṟu tōṉṟiyapōtu, atu, itu, utu eṉṉum cuṭṭuppeyarkaḷ oṉṟaṉpā līṟāyiṉa.
a-am-aṉ-al-atu; i-im-iṉ-il-itu; u-um-uṉ-ul-utu.
e-ṭu: (kiḻatu)-kiḻaṭu, karitu-karicu, ērutu-erutu.
ciṟitu peritu eḷitu valitu eṉṉum coṟkaḷiṉ īṟu, itu eṉṉum cuṭṭuppeyarē. 
valatti, karutti, ciṟutti, venti eṉṉum coṟkaḷ, muṟiyē, valattatu, ciṟuttatu, ventatu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvati yum, aikāravīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ pala mutalil akara vīṟṟavāyirun tatiyum, nōkkumpōtu, akaramum atu eṉṉum cuṭṭaṭi yīṟupōṟ payaṉpaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu karuta iṭamēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu.
palaviṉpālīṟukaḷ
mutaṟkālattilirunta paṉmiyīṟu, kūṭutal eṉṉum poruḷ koṇṭa um eṉṉum colleṉpatu, muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭatu. aṟamum poruḷum eṉṉun toṭaril, um eṉpatu kūṭutaṟ poruḷik kuṟittal kāṇka. ac col iṉṟu ap poruḷil vaḻakkaṟṟup pōyiṉum, ataṉ aṭippiṟanta kum eṉṉuñ col ap poruḷil vaḻaṅkutali nōkkuka.
kummutal kūṭutal, kum-kummiṅ, kum-kumpu - kumpal
ummīṟṟiṉpiṉ tōṉṟiya paṉmiyīṟu kaḷ eṉpatē. atuvum kūṭutal eṉṉum poruḷatē.
kaḷḷutal = kūṭutal, kalattal, poruntutal, ottal. kaḷ - kaḷam = kūṭṭam, kūṭumiṭam. avikkaḷam, ērkkaḷam, pōrkkaḷam, tiṇikkaḷam mutaliya coṟkaḷi nōkkuka. kaḷam - kaḷaṉ - kaḷam -kaḷar - kaḷari. kaḷam - (kaḷaku) - kaḻaku - kaḻakam.
kaḷḷa eṉpatu ōr uvamavurupu.
kaḷḷa matippa vella vīḻa		      (tol. 1285)
kaḷḷa=porunta, okka.
palaporuḷkaḷ kūṭutal eṉṉuṅ karuttil, kaḷ eṉṉuñ col paṉmiyīṟāyiṟṟu. maraṅkaḷ = marakkūṭṭam.
aimpālīṟu tōṉṟiyapōtu, akaraccuṭṭum ataṉaṭip piṟanta avi eṉṉum colliṉ viyīṟum palaviṉpālīṟāka vari yaṟukkap peṟṟaṉa.
a-am-av-(ava)-avi.
i-im-iv-(iva)-ivi.
u-um-uv-(uva)-uvi.
e-ṭu: pala, nallavi.
pālpakā aḵṟiṇippeyar
orumiyīṟum paṉmiyīṟum peṟātu iyalpāka irukkum aḵṟiṇip peyarkaḷellām, orumikkum paṉmikkum potuvām. avaṟṟiṉ eṇ, avaṟṟiṉ viṉimuṟṟālum muṉpiṉ varum collālum aṟiyappaṭum. itu vaḻāniliyām.
e-ṭu: maram vaḷarkiṟatu.
       	kutiri ōṭukiṟatu.        		orumi
       	oru kāy eṉṉavili?
        	maram vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa.
       	kutiri ōṭukiṉṟaṉa.       	     	paṉmi
	nūṟukāy vāṅkiṉēṉ.
uyartiṇiyilum, āṇpālīṟum peṇpālīṟum peṟātu av viraṇṭiṟkum potuvāyirukkum orucila peyarkaḷ im muṭipu koḷḷum. itu vaḻuvamitiyām.
e-ṭu:	pērukku aintuvarum.   - orumi
āyirampērik koṉṟavaṉ ari maruttuvaṉ. - paṉmi
peṟṟatāyip pēṇāta mūṭar eṉpatil, tāy eṉa orumiyil vantatu vakupporumi yeṉappaṭum. tāyar eṉṟu paṉmiyil variṉ, ovvoruvarkkum naṟṟāyar palar eṉṟu poruḷpaṭutalum  aṅṅaṉam kūṭāmiyum kāṇka.
paṇpuppeyarīṟukaḷ
paṇpuppeyarīṟukaḷ cuṭṭaṭiccol, ciṉippeyar, iṭappeya r, nīrppeyar eṉṉum nālvakiyil tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
cuṭṭaṭiccol
a-ai	e-ṭu: 	tolli, pacci
a-am	   	nalam, ciṉam, āḻam, taṉam(taṉmi)
am-aṉ	    	tiṟam-tiṟaṉ
aṉ-al	    	tiṟaṉ-tiṟal
al	 	iyal
antu+ai                		aranti
atu-tu	    	cētu (civappu)
tu-ṟu	    	naṉṟu
atu-ati-ti	    	maṟati, amiti
ti-ṟi	   	naṉṟi
ti-ci	   	māṭci, vaṟaṭci
al-aḷ	    	mañcal-mañcaḷ
aḷ-aṇ	    	muraṇ
al-ar	    	mayal-mayar
ar	    	naṉṉar
am-av-avu	    	maḻavu
avu-avi-vi	    	maṟavi
avu-apu-pu	    	māṇpu, paṇpu, aṉpu
pu+am	    	nuṇ-nuṭpu-nuṭpam, iṉ-iṉpam
al+pu	    	iyalpu
avu-aku	 	kuḻavu-kuḻaku
aku-ku		naṉku, aḻaku
iṅṅaṉamē ēṉi yirucuṭṭiṟkum oṭṭikkoḷka.
e-ṭu : i - vekuḷi, il-eḻil (aḻaku), itam - perumitam.
ciṉippeyar
e-ṭu: taṟukaṇ (añcāmi).
iṭappeyar
e-ṭu: akam-vañcakam, tali-uṟutali, kaṇ-iṭukkaṇ.
nīrppeyar
         nīr - neṭunīr (maṟavi), mi-taṉmi, naṉmi.
kuṇaṅkaḷ perumpālum viṉivāyilāy veḷippaṭuva tāl kuṇappeyarkaḷum perumpālum viṉiyaṭip peyar kaḷākavēyuḷḷaṉa.
iṉippu puḷippu mutaliyaṉa collāl toḻiṟpeyarā yiṉum, poruḷāṟ paṇpuppeyarām.
añcāmiyum maṟamum kaṇṇāl veḷippaṭutaliṉ, ak kuṇattik kuṟikkak kaṇ eṉṉum col īṟāyiṟṟu. kaṭuṅkaṇ maṟavar eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
kaṇ kāl ki tali mutaliya ciṉippeyarkaḷ, iṭap peyarākavum iruvaki vaḻakkilum āḷappeṟum.
tamiḻnāṭu veppanāṭātaliṉ, kuḷircciyit tarum nīriṉ peyarum maḻiyiṉ peyarum iṉiya taṉmiyik kuṟikkalāyiṉa. piṉṉar avi taṉmi eṉṉum potupporuḷil āḷappeṟṟaṉa.
kakki karavā karintuṇṇum ākkamum
 aṉṉanī rārkkē yuḷa.  	(527)
cīrmi ciṟappoṭu nīṅkum payaṉila
 nīrmi yuṭiyār coliṉ.  	(195)
neṭuṅkaṭalun taṉṉīrmi kuṉṟum taṭinteḻili
 tāṉalkā tāki viṭiṉ.    				   (17)
eṉṉum kuṟaḷkaḷi nōkkuka.
nīr = nīriṉ kuḷirnta taṉmi, taṉmi. nīrmi = ciṟanta taṉmi - taṉmi. mi = karumukil, maḻinīr, nīr.
nīr, mi eṉṉum irucollum paṇpuppeyarīṟāṉapiṉ, nīr eṉṉum collum taṉmi yeṉṉum potuppaṇpup peyar pōl miyīṟu peṟṟateṉka.
toḻiṟpeya rīṟukaḷ
toḻiṟpeyar vakikaḷ
(1)  mutaṉilit toḻiṟpeyar. e-ṭu: aṭi, kaṭṭu.
(2)  mutaṉili nīṇṭa toḻiṟpeyar.
	e-ṭu: uṇ - ūṇ, puṟappaṭu - puṟappāṭu, kūppiṭu - 		kūppīṭu - kūppāṭu.                                   
(3)  mutaṉili valitta toḻiṟpeyar.
	e-ṭu: virumpu - viruppu, nīntu - nīttu.
(4)  mutaṉili valiyiraṭṭitta toḻiṟpeyar.
	e-ṭu: karutu, karuttu, pēcu-pēccu.
(5)  mutaṉili īṟupeṟṟa toḻiṟpeyar.
	e-ṭu: ceyki, paṭippu.
(6)  mutaṉili nīṇṭu īṟupeṟṟa toḻiṟpeyar.
	e-ṭu: naṭi- nāṭakam, paṭi-pāṭam.
(7)  mutaṉili valiyiraṭṭittu īṟupeṟṟa toḻiṟpeyar.
	e-ṭu: āṭu-āṭṭam, āku-ākkam.
āṭṭam, ākkam eṉpaṉa āṭutal, ākutal eṉṟu poruḷ paṭuṅkāl, taṉviṉiyaṭip piṟantaviyē.
 (8)  mutaṉili valittu īṟupeṟṟa toḻiṟpeyar.
	e-ṭu: añcu-accu-accam, virumpu-viruppu-viruppam.
 (9)  īṟu tirinta toḻiṟpeyar.
	e-ṭu: vel-veṉ, vēḷ-vēṇ.
(10)  palavīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar.
	e-ṭu: kala-kalappu, kalappaṭam, yā-yāppu-yāppuṟavu.
toḻiṟpeyarīṟu vakikaḷ
(1) ciṉippeyarkaḷ, (2) iṭappeyarkaḷ, (3) cuṭṭaṭikaḷ,     (4) aḷavukuṟitta coṟkaḷ, (5) paṇpuppeyarīṟu. (6) viṉimuṟṟīṟu.
(1) ciṉippeyarkaḷ
ki eṉpatu ākupeyarāyk kiyiṉāṟ ceyyum toḻili yuṅ kuṟikkum. itu karuviyākupeyar.
iravār irappārkkoṉ ṟīvar karavātu
 kiceytūṇ māli yavar	(1035)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷiluḷḷa `kiceytu' eṉṉun toṭarukku, kiyiṉāṟ ceytu eṉṟu uri kūṟuvatiṉum, ciṟanta toḻilākiya uḻavic ceytu kūṟuvatu poruttamām.
toḻilik kuṟikkum ki eṉṉum col toḻiṟ peyarīṟāvatu poruttamē.
e-ṭu: cey-ceyki, kal-kaṟki, nampu-nampikki.
ki eṉṉum ciṉippeyarum cey eṉṉum viṉiyiṉiṉṟu tirintatē . cey-(key)-ki.
toḻiṟpeya rīṟāṉa maṟṟoru kippeyar pāṇi eṉpatām.
e-ṭu: ciri-cirippāṇi=cirikki (cirippu).
itu nelli vaṭṭāra vaḻakku. pāṇi ki. paṇṇuvatu pāṇi. kiyiṉāṟ ceyyum icik kālavaṟuppum pāṇi yeṉappaṭum.
(2) iṭappeyarkaḷ
akam-nampakam, vañcakam, tāṇṭavam-tāṇṭakam.
tali-viṭutali.
cuṭṭaṭiccoṟkum iṭappeyarkkum vēṟupāṭaṟital vēṇṭum. akam eṉpatu cuṭṭaṭic collāyiṉ aku+am eṉṟu piriyum; iṭap peyarāyiṉ piriyātu.
e.ṭu: kaḻaku-kaḻakam, ulaku-ulakam.
nampu-nampakam, vañci-vañcakam, naṭi-nāṭakam.

(4) vēṟṟumi yurupukaḷ
moḻi potumakkaḷ amippātaliṉ, ataṉ iṉṟiyami yāta kūṟukaḷāṉa vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷum avarkaḷ amittaṉavē. vēṟṟumikaḷi ēḻeṉṟum eṭṭeṉṟum variyaṟuttum, avaṟṟi varicippaṭuttiyatum, avaṟṟiṟkup peyariṭṭatum, karuvi vēṟṟumiyiyum uṭaṉikaḻcci vēṟṟumiyiyum oṉṟucērttu oru vēṟṟumi yākkiyatumē, mutaṉūlāciriyaṉum  vaḻinūlāciriyaṉumāṉa  ilakkaṇiyar ceyta viṉikaḷām. ivaṟṟiṉ viḷakkatti eṉ  `tolkāppiya viḷakkam' eṉṉum nūliṟ kāṇka.
vēṟṟumi yeṭṭum allatu oṉpatum, ilakkaṇanūlār vakutta variciyoḻuṅkil tōṉṟiyirukka muṭiyātu. avvav
vēṟṟumik karuttut tōṉṟiyapōtu avvav vēṟṟumiyurupu tōṉṟiyiruttalvēṇṭum.
oruvaṉ mutalil tāṉē viṉimutalāyiruntu oṉṟu ceyvatē iyalpātalālum, oruvaṉ varalāṟṟi ōrūril oruvaṉ iruntāṉ' eṉṟu toṭaṅkuvatē marapātalālum, mutalāvatu eḻuvāyk karuttut tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. ataṟku urupu tēviyilli; iyalpāṉa peyarē pōtum. tirimoḻikaḷilēyē eḻuvāy vēṟṟumikkum urupu amikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. tamiḻ iyaṉmoḻi yeṉpataṟku, atil eḻuvāyurupiṉmiyum oru cāṉṟām.
oruvaṉ oruttiyuṭaṉ allatu iṉṉoruvaṉuṭaṉ pēcu muṉ, avira viḷikkātu irukkamuṭiyātu. itu moḻitōṉṟāta niliyilum nikaḻum.
oruvaṉ oru viṉi ceyyiṉum oṉṟum ceyyātu cummā viruppiṉum, avaṉ irukka ōr iṭamvēṇṭum. ātalāl, iṭak karuttu aṭuttut tōṉṟiyirukkalām.
malikkukiyil vatinta anākarika māntaṉukkum niliyāṉa taṉiyiṭam vēṇṭiyiruntatiṉāl, uṭimik karuttut tōṉṟi yirukkum.
māntaṉ viṉikaḷuḷ, cilavaṟṟiṟku oṉṟum tēviyilli; cilavaṟṟiṟku ētēṉum vēṇṭum. eḻutalum naṭattalum tāmāka nikaḻum. āyiṉ paṟittalum uṇṭalum kāykaṉipōṉṟaviyiṉṟi nikaḻā. uṇṇutal uyirvāḻkkikku iṉṟiyamiyāta viṉi. oṉṟum putitāyc ceyyāta anākarikak kālattilum māntaṉ iyaṟkiyuṇavi uṇṭuvantāṉ. ataṉāl, ceyporuṭ karuttut tōṉṟiyirukkum.
kiḻaṅkit tōṇṭuvataṟkum vilaṅku  paṟavikaḷik kolvataṟkum, kallum kōlum pōṉṟa karuvikaḷ tēvippaṭṭa pōtu, karuvik karuttut tōṉṟiyirukkum.
paṟittum akaḻntum vēṭṭiyāṭiyum koṇṭu vanta poruḷkaḷi, maṉivimakkaṭkum uṟṟār uṟaviṉarkkum koṭuttapōtu, koṭikkaruttut tōṉṟiyirukkum.
maramēṟik kaṉipaṟittavaṉum maliyēṟit tēṉeṭuttavaṉum, kīḻiṟaṅkiyapōtu allatu tavaṟi viḻuntapōtu, allatu kāykaṉi marattiṉiṉṟu kīḻē viḻuntapōtu, nīkkak karuttut tōṉṟi yirukkum.
māntaṉ kūṭivāḻum uyiriyātalāl, ōriṭattiliruppiṉum, oṉṟic ceyyiṉum, ōriṭam celliṉum, peṟṟōruṭaṉ allatu maṉiviyuṭaṉ allatu makkaḷuṭaṉ allatu nāyuṭaṉ (allatu karuviyuṭaṉ) irukkavum ceyyavum cellavum nērntapōtu, uṭaṉikaḻccik karuttut tōṉṟiyirukkum.
iṅṅaṉam eṇ allatu toṇvēṟṟumikaḷum, kuṟiñci vāḻkki niliyilēyē muntutamiḻar moḻiyil tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷiṉ varalāṟu
uruttal tōṉṟutal. uru=tōṟṟam, vaṭivam, uṭampu, taṉipporuḷ. uru-uruvu-uruvam. uruvu-urupu=vēṟṟumi vaṭiva māṉa col allatu aci.

5ām vēṟṟumiyurupu (nīkkam)
iṉ. itu iṭapporuḷurupāṉa il eṉpataṉ tiripē.
iliruntu (il+iruntu) allatu iṉiṉṟu (il+niṉṟu) eṉṉum ulakavaḻakkuk kūṭṭuccollurupē, ceyyuḷvaḻakkil il eṉa taṉic collurupāyk kuṟukit tirintatu. iṉiṉṟu eṉṉum puṇarcciyum itaṟkup peritum utaviṟṟu.
marattiliruntu, marattiṉiṉṟu eṉṉum ulaka vaḻakkē, iyaṟkiyākavum poruḷnirampiyum mūlattit teḷivāyk kāṭṭuvatākavum iruttal kāṇka. iṟaṅkum allatu viḻumnili, iruttal niṟṟal ākiya iraṇṭil oṉṟākavē yirukkum.

7ām vēṟṟumiyurupu (iṭam)
iṭiccollāka niṉṟu iṭapporuḷuṇarttum ellāc coṟkaḷum 7ām vēṟṟumiyurupām. vēṟṟumi yeṉṟatu poruḷpaṟṟiyē yaṉṟic coṟpaṟṟiyaṉṟu. āyiṉ, āriyam pōṉṟa tirimoḻikaḷil vēṟṟumi colliyē taḻuviniṟkum.
iṭapporuḷurupukaḷuḷ, ciṟappākak koḷḷappeṟuvatu ulakavaḻakkil il; ceyyuḷ vaḻakkil kaṇ.
iṭam, pakkam, ōram, naṭu, uḷ, veḷi, kīḻ. mēl, muṉ, piṉ ākiya ellāvaki yiṭapporuḷilum 7ām vēṟṟumi yurupukaḷ varum. iṭam eṉṉum collum illurupēṟpa tuṇṭu.
e-ṭu: eṉṉiṭam, eṉṉiṭattil.
8ām vēṟṟumiyurupu (viḷi)
peyarkaḷ viḷikkappaṭum niliyil aṭiyum vaṭivē 8ām vēṟṟumiyurupām. av vaṭivu peyarkaḷiṉ īṟṟip poṟuttatu. viḷiyēṟṟa peyarkaḷ perumpālum tiriyum, ciṟupāṉmi tiriyā.
tiriyum peyarkaḷ cēymic cuṭṭāyiṉ, aḷapeṭukkum; tiriyāp peyarkaḷ cēymic cuṭṭāyiṉ, avaṟṟiṟku muṉ aḷapeṭutta ē allatu ō eṉṉum viḷiyoli cērkkappeṟum; ap peyarkaḷiṉ  īṟum ciṟupāṉmi aḷapeṭukkum. tiriyum peyarkku muṉṉum ē allatu ō cērkkappeṟuvatumuṇṭu.
e-ṭu: iyalpuviḷi: pēriṉpam, nampikki.
tiripuviḷi : īṟu mikutal	-      	teyvamē, makaṉē

ella eṉṉum viḷiccol
viḷiyolikaḷuḷ, ella eṉpatu toṉṟutoṭṭu varuvatum, iruvaki vaḻakkilum vaḻaṅkuvatum, ilakkaṇattil iṭampeṟṟatum tamiḻiṉ muṉmiyiyum piṟamoḻikaḷiṉ piṉmiyiyum uṇarttu vatum, āṅkilattiṟkum tamiḻukkumuḷḷa aṇukkattik kāṭṭuvatum ākum.

 nilikkuri marapiṉ iruvīṟṟum urittē	(1166)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam kūṟum. itu ellā eṉṟu īṟu nīṇṭu ellē eṉṟum tirintatu.
nillāṅku nillāṅ kivartaral ellānī		(30)
eṉṉum marutakkaliyil, ellā eṉpatu talivi talivaṉi viḷittatu.
ellā viḵtottaṉ eṉpeṟāṉ kēṭṭikkāṇ	(25)
eṉṉum kuṟiñcikkaliyil ellā eṉpatu tōḻi taliviyi viḷittatu.
“všny!-JobfhËil maṭattōḻī		(5:3:5)
eṉṉun tiruvāymoḻi yaṭiyil , ellē eṉpaṉatu talivi tōḻiyi viḷittatu.
ellā eṉpatu piṉpu ēlā eṉṟu mutal nīṇṭatu. 
kuṟavaṉ makaḷāṇi kūṟalō kūṟēl		(8:69)
eṉṉum paripāṭalaṭiyil ēlā eṉpatu tōḻi talivaṉi viḷittatu.
karūrppakkattil, kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyi ēlā eṉṟu viḷippatu, iṉṟum kallā makkaḷiṭi vaḻakkamāyirukkiṉṟatu.
nelli nāṭṭār, ciṟuvariyum kīḻōrāṉa āṭavariyum iṉṟu  ēla, ēlē eṉṟu viḷikkiṉṟaṉar. kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyi ēḻā eṉṟu viḷippatu an nāṭṭuk kīḻōr vaḻakkam. ēla-ēḻa-ēḻā.
ēḻa eṉpatu piṉpu ēṭa eṉṟu tirintatu.
ēṭā aḻiyal eḻuntitu koḷḷāy		(14:12)
eṉṉum maṇimēkaliyaṭiyil, ēṭā eṉpatu cintātēvi āputtiraṉiviḷittatu. ēṭa-ēṭā ivi āṭūu (āṇpāl)viḷi. ēṭi, ēṭī eṉpaṉa makaṭūu (peṇpāl) viḷi.
ēṭa eṉpatu piṉpu aṭa-aṭā-aṭē eṉat tirintatu. it tiripukaḷum āṭūu viḷiyām. aṭi-aṭī eṉpaṉa makaṭūu viḷiyām.
nilla ṭīiyeṉak kaṭukiṉaṉ peṇṇeṉa niṉintāṉ 
(kamparā. āraṇi. 93)
eṉpatil aṭī eṉpatu ilakkumaṇaṉ kurppaṇakiyi viḷittatu.
aṭa, aṭā eṉṉum coṟkaḷ kaḻivirakkak kuṟippākavum vaḻaṅkum.
aṭā eṉpatu teluṅkil arā-rā eṉṟum, aṭē eṉpatu vaṭamoḻiyil arē-rē eṉṟum tirintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. intiyil aṭē eṉpatu arē-rē eṉṟum, aṭaṭa eṉpatu arara eṉṟum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ṭa-ra. pōlittiripu. o.nō: kuṭampi-kurampi, paṭavar-paravar.
ella eṉṉum viḷiyiṭiccolliliruntu, cila peyarc coṟkaḷum piṟantuḷḷaṉa.
ella-eluvaṉ = tōḻaṉ. eluvaṉ - eluval. eluvi = tōḻi. ēṭa-ēṭaṉ=aṭiyāṉ.
ellā eṉṉum tamiḻccollum, hallo eṉṉum āṅkilac collum, oliyum poruḷum ottiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu. iv voppukikkuk karaṇiyum aṭutta paṭalattil viḷakkappeṟum.
mutaṟkālattil, vēḷum vēḷiṉiyumākiya talivaṉum taliviyum oruvariyoruvar matippāka viḷitta ellā eṉṉum col ēla, eḻa, ēṭa, aṭa, aṭā, aṭē, aṭī eṉap paṭippaṭiyāy vaṭivutirintum matippukkuṉṟiyum pālpirintum, nāḷaṭivil iḻi makkaḷi viḷikkum collāyiṟṟu.
cila vēṟṟumi marapukaḷ
uṭaṉikaḻcciyi oru taṉi vēṟṟumiyākkiṉ, vēṟṟumi mottam oṉpatām.
ovvoru vēṟṟumiyum, ovvoru allatu orucila iṭiccollāl uṇarttappeṟum palvēṟu karuttukaḷiṉ tokutiyēyaṉṟi oru taṉippaṭṭa karuttaṉṟu. iraṇṭām vēṟṟumip poruḷāka irupatteṇ karuttukaḷi eṭuttuk kūṟiṉār tolkāppiyar. āyiṉum, avaṟṟuḷ ellām aṭaṅkavilli. ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟāviṉi eṉa ilak kaṇiyar vakutta viṉic colvakikku, ēṟṟa karuttukaḷellām iraṇṭām vēṟṟumip poruḷē allatu ceyporuḷ vakiyē. iṅṅaṉamē ēṉi vēṟṟumikaḷum karuttuttokuti kaḷē eṉa aṟika.
aciyurupu collurupu eṉa vēṟṟumiyurupu iruvakip paṭum. cila karuttukaḷic collurupāṟ kuṟi ruppatē ulaka vaḻakkiṟ peruvaḻakkām. collurupu ētēṉumō raciyurupoṭu kūṭi varuvatē perumpāṉmi.
vēṟṟumi       	poruḷ        	collurupu	eṭuttukkāṭṭu

5āvatu          	uṟaḻporuḷ  	ai+viṭa      	ativiṭa
                          		ai+kāṭṭilum   	atikkāṭṭilum
6āvatu            	uṭimi    	uṭiya    	kaṇṇaṉuṭiya
uṭimik karutti vēṟṟumittokiyāṟ kuṟippatē ulaka vaḻakkiṟ perumpāṉmiyām. mūviṭap pakarappeyarkaḷ iv vaḻakkil neṭumutal kuṟukum.
e-ṭu: maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ māḷiki, eṉ makaḷ.
aciyurupāṟ poruḷ nirampātapōtu oru collum ataṉuṭaṉ  cērkkappeṟum.
e-ṭu: avaṉ eṉakkāka iṅku vantāṉ.
orē poruḷ, colvāṉ karuttuppaṟṟiyum muṭipuc col paṟṟiyum, vevvēṟu vēṟṟumiyāl kuṟikkappeṟum.
e-ṭu:
añciyiṉ makaṉ poruṭṭeḻiṉi (uṭimi)
añcikku makaṉ poruṭṭeḻiṉi  (uṟavumuṟi)
tiruvaḷḷuvari nikarppār yār? (viṉimuṭipu)
tiruvaḷḷuvari nikar yār?   (peyarmuṭipu)
oru vēṟṟumi vēṟoru vēṟṟumiyiyun tuṇik koḷḷum.
e-ṭu: aṟikkuḷ eṉpatil 7āvatu 4āvatit tuṇik koṇṭami kāṇka.
(5) cāriyikaḷ
tāmākac cērāta colluṟuppukaḷiyum coṟkaḷiyum cārntu iyikkum acikaḷum coṟkaḷum, cāriyi eṉappaṭum. iyittal icittal. tāmāka olikkāta eḻuttukaḷi olittaṟkum, olikkum eḻuttukaḷi eḷitāy olittaṟkum, avaṟṟic cārntuvarum olikaḷum cāriyi eṉappaṭum. ākavē. eḻuttuc cāriyi, coṟcāriyi eṉac cāriyi iruvakiyām.
eḻuttuccāriyi
uyireḻuttukaḷuḷ kuṟilukkuk karamum neṭilukkuk kāramum cāriyiyām. neṭilkaḷuḷ ai, au eṉṉum iraṇṭiṟkum kāṉ eṉpatu ciṟappuccāriyi.
āytattiṟkuc cāriyi ēṉam eṉpatām. atu cērumpōtu āytattiṟku muṉ akaramum piṉ kakarameyyum cērntu aḵkēṉam eṉṟākum.
meyyeḻuttiṟku a' cāriyi. atu meykkuppiṉ varum. ka eṉpatu cāriyi yēṟṟa meyyeḻuttiṟkum ka eṉṉum kuṟilukkum potuvāyiruppatāl, meyyeḻutti vitantu kuṟikkumpōtu kakaramey eṉpatu marapu.
uyirmeyyeḻuttukaḷuḷ, kuṟilukkuk karam cāriyi; neṭilukkut taṉiccāriyi illi. ataṉāl meyyiyum neṭiliyum pirittuk kakara ākāram (kā), kakara īkāram (kī) eṉṟa muṟiyiṟ collappeṟum.
coṟcāriyi
a, attu, am, aṟṟu, aṉ, ām, iṟṟu, iṉ, u, ai mutaliyaṉa coṟcāriyi.
e-ṭu:	taṭṭārappāṭṭam, eṉakku, paṭṭiṉattāṉ, kuḷattup pāyccal, puḷiyampaḻam, avaṟṟi, ataṉi, kallāṅ koḷḷi, patiṟṟuppattu, patiṉoṉṟu, vēriṉi, avaṉukku, paṇṭik kālam.
ivaṟṟuḷ, aṟṟuccāriyip puṇarcciyum iṟṟuccāriyip puṇarcciyum iṉṟu ulaka vaḻakkaṟṟaṉa. avikaḷ eṉpatu miki paṭakkūṟum vaḻūuc collum, atukaḷ eṉpatu iḻivaḻakkum ākum. ātalāl, avaṟṟi ivaṟṟi evaṟṟi eṉṟē kumari nāṭṭup potumakkaḷ vaḻaṅkiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
cāriyikaḷum cuṭṭaṭiyiṉavē.

	aṉ, al etirkālattil maṭṭum varum.
(2)	ceytu eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu iṟantakāla viṉiyiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyavi

(3)	etirkāla viṉimuṟṟiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatu.
	ceyyum+ēṉ = (ceyyumēṉ) - ceyyuvēṉ - ceyvēṉ-	ceykēṉ - ceyku - ku.
	va-ka, pōli. o. nō: āvā-ākā, civappu-cikappu.
(4)	ukaraccuṭṭaṭiyāyp piṟantatu (etirkālam)
       	utu.  e-ṭu: ceyyutu, kūṟutu, varutu.
utu īṟu tuvvaḷavākak kuṟuki, aṭinīṇṭa lakara ḷakaravīṟṟu viṉimutaṉilikaḷōṭu kūṭumpōtu, ṟukara ṭukaramākat tiriyum.

          	koḷ-kōḷ+tu = kōṭu.
o.nō: cel-cēl+tal = cēṟal (toḻiṟpeyar)
 	  koḷ-kōḷ+tal = kōṭal (toḻiṟpeyar).
taṉmippaṉmi yīṟukaḷ
(1)	taṉmip paṉmip peyarkaḷiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyavi. 
	ē-em, (nām) - ām-am, ām-ōm.
(2) ceytu eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu iṟantakāla viṉiyiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyavi.
	tu+um (paṉmiyīṟu)=tum.
	tum - ṭum. tum - ṟum
(3)	etirkāla viṉimuṟṟiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatu.
	ku+um (paṉmiyīṟu)=kum.
(4) ukarntāy piṟantatu (etirkālam),
	e-ṭu: ceyyutum, kūṟutum, varutum.
	tu-ṟu. cēṟu+um (paṉmiyīṟu)=cēṟum.
	tu-ṭu. kōṭu+um(paṉmiyīṟu)=kōṭum.
(5) ceyyum eṉṉum muṟṟiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatu.
	ceyyum-um.

nīm ceytīm eṉpatu iṉṟum teṉṉāṭṭu vaḻakku.

ēvalviṉi yīṟukaḷ
	orumi:	(1)	īṟaṟṟatu. e-ṭu: cey.

			cey+utu+ī=ceyyutī-ceyti.
vaḻipaṭuvōri vallaṟitīyē 		(10:1)
eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭil varum aṟitī eṉpataṟku, aṟivi eṉṟu paḻiyavuri poruḷ kūṟiyiruppatiyum, aṟitī-aṟiti = aṟivāy eṉṟu cāminātiyar aruñcoṟkuṟippu varintiruppatiyum, ūṉṟi nōkkuka.
ceṉṟī (ceṉṟu+ī) eṉpatu ceṉṟutavuka eṉṟu poruḷ paṭum utaviviṉi. niṉmē (nillum+ē) eṉpatu nilluṅkaḷēṉ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum ārvaviṉi. nillum eṉpatu ukarantokiṉ niṉm eṉṟākum. o-nō: pōlum-pōṉm. ēṉ eṉpatu eṉak kuṟintu niṉṟatu. ulakavaḻakkilum  varaṭṭē eṉa vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka. niṉ+mē eṉṉum tavaṟāṉa pakuppu, ati orumiyeṉak koḷḷac ceytirukkiṉṟatu. itiyaṟiyātu,
muṉṉili muṉṉar īyum ēyum
annili marapiṉ meyyūrntu varumē		(934)
eṉa nūṟpā yāttār tolkāppiyar. atip piṉpaṟṟiṉār pavaṇantiyār.
(3) ceyyāy eṉṉum muṉṉili yorumiviṉi kural vēṟupāṭṭāl, cey eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum. āy eṉpatu ēva lorumi yīṟeṉṟē kūṟātu,
ceyyāy eṉṉum muṉṉili viṉiccol
 ceyyeṉ kiḷavi ākiṭaṉ uṭittē	(933)
eṉṟē tolkāppiyar kūṟuvatāl, ataṉ arukiya vaḻakkup pulaṉākum. āy eṉpatu muṉṉili yorumi yīṟēyāyiṉum, ēvalorumi viṉi ati ēṟkavēṇṭumeṉṟum yāppuṟa villi, eṉa aṟika.
paṉmi 
(1)	muṉṉilip paṉmip peyariṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyavi
	(ūm) - um.		e-ṭu : ceyyum
	um+kaḷ.		e-ṭu : ceyyuṅkaḷ
	um+(nīm+īm-im)-iṉ.	e-ṭu : ceyyumiṉ-ceymmiṉ
           	miṉ+kaḷ 		e-ṭu : ceymmiṉkaḷ
miṉ eṉṉum īṟṟiṉ makaram neṟi (viti) mutal eṉa aṟika.
kaḷ eṉpatu paṭarkki yīṟēṉum iraṭṭip paṉmi kuṟittu mūviṭattum varum.
e-ṭu: nāṅkaḷ, nīṅkaḷ, avarkaḷ.
ceyyumiṉ eṉpatiluḷḷa ummiṉ (um+iṉ) eṉṉum iraṭṭippaṉmiyi, ceyyuṅkaḷ eṉpatiluḷḷa uṅkaḷ (um+kaḷ) eṉpatupōlak koḷka. ceymmiṉ eṉpataṉ iraṭṭippaṉmip poruḷ maṟintapiṉ, kaḷḷīṟu piṟavaṟ ṟoṭu cērntatupōl ataṉōṭum cērntatu. im mukkip paṉmiyip peṇ peṇṭāṭṭi eṉaptupōṉṟa mīmicic collākak koḷka.
(2) etirkāla viṉimuṟṟiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatu.
        cey+utu+īr = ceyyutīr-ceyyutirceytir.
(3) ceyyāy eṉpataṟkottu collum vaṭivam.

piṟaviṉi yīṟukaḷ
taṉviṉi piṟaviṉiyākum vakikaḷ
(1) mutaṉili valittal
	e-ṭu: nīṅku-nīkku, poruntu-poruttu
(2) iṭinili valittal
	e-ṭu: tēyntatu-tēyttatu, tīrntāṉ-tīrttāṉ.
(3) mutaṉili valiyiraṭṭal
	e-ṭu: pōku-pōkku, tēṟu-tēṟṟu.
(4) īṟupeṟutal
	ī-vī-vi-pi.
	ceyya+ī = ceyyavī-ceyvī-ceyvi.
	naṭappa+ī = naṭappavī-naṭappī-naṭappi.
	ceyyavīttāṉ = ceyyavutaviṉāṉ, ceyyaviṭṭāṉ, ceyyac ceytāṉ.
īttuvakkum iṉpam			(kuṟaḷ. 228)
emakkīt taṉiyē		(puṟam. 911) 
eṉa ītalviṉi valittum iṟantakālaṅ kāṭṭutal kāṇka.
vi, pi, piṟaviṉi yīṟukaḷāṉapiṉ, viṉikaḷ avaṟṟi ēṟṟa peṟṟi oṉṟum palavum ēṟṟaṉa.
ceyvippi, naṭattuvi-irumaṭip piṟaviṉi.
ceyvippippi,naṭattu vippa-mummaṭip piṟaviṉi.
ivi ceyvippittāṉ, ceyvippippittāṉ eṉa nikaḻcci viṉiyām. ellā viṉikaḷum mutaṉiliyaḷavil ēvalvaṭivā yiruttaliṉ, piṟaviṉi mutaṉilikaḷi ēvalviṉiyeṉṟu vitantukūṟa vēṇṭiyatilli.
ottu-attu-tu. ottu-oṭṭu-aṭṭu. ottutal=poruntutal, oṟṟutal, icital.
o-a. o-nō; kompu-kampu. moṇṭi-maṇṭi, mottiki - mattiki.
vāḻa+attu = (vāḻattu)-vāḻttu-vaḻuttu.
tāḻa+attu = (tāḻattu)-tāḻttu.
oṭṭu-aṭṭu. vara+aṭṭu = varaṭṭu.
attu eṉṉum tuṇiviṉi tuvvīṟākak kuṟintapiṉ, ataṉ tiripākak cu ṭu ṟu īṟukaḷ tōṉṟiṉa.
e-ṭu:	pāy+tu = pāyttu-pāyccu.
	nīḷ+tu = nīṭṭu, kāṇ+tu = kāṭṭu.
         nāl+tu = nāṟṟu, tiṉ+tu = tīṟṟu.
orucila viṉikaḷ uvuīṟu peṟṟup piṟaviṉiyākiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu: eḻu-eḻuvu, koḷ-koḷuvu.
eḻuvutal=eḻacceytal, ōciyeḻupputal.
koḷuvutal= koḷḷac ceytal.
iv uvu īṟu ovvu eṉṉum tuṇiviṉiyiṉ citivākum. ovvutal=ottal, poruntutal, icital.
eḻa+ovvu=eḻuvu, koḷa+ovvu+koḷuvu, ukarattiṉ muṉ akaram tokkatu.
(5) tuṇiviṉi peṟutal
e-ṭu: 	ūṟavi, kāyappōṭu, niṟpāṭṭu, maṟakkaṭi, eḻaccey, varappaṇṇu.
(6) aḷapeṭuttal
ikkālattil kaṭṭu, maṇṭu mutaliya kuṟṟukaravīṟṟu viṉic coṟkaḷ kaṭṭi, maṇṭi eṉa ikaravīṟēṟṟu iṟantakāla viṉi yeccamāvatu pōl, mutaṟkālattil iru, uṭu mutaliya muṟṟukara vīṟṟu viṉiccoṟkaḷum ikaravīṟēṟṟu iri, uṭi eṉa iṟanta kālaviṉi yeccamāki yirukkalām. iv vaṭivaṅkaḷi aḷa peṭippaṭuttip piṟaviṉiyākki yirukkalām.
iri-irīi=irutti, uṭi-uṭīi=uṭutti, koḷi-koḷīi= koḷutti.
koḷi(koḷḷi) eṉpati aḷḷi, eḷḷi, taḷḷi, tuḷḷi eṉpaṉapōṟ koḷka.
(7) iyalpāyiruttal
e-ṭu:	katavu tiṟantatu-katavit tiṟantāṉ, viṟaku piḷantatu-	viṟakip piḷantāṉ.

peyarecca vīṟukaḷ
terinilip peyareccam:
a - i. kā. īṟu. e-ṭu: ceyta ( ceytu+a)
a - ni. kā. īṟu. e-ṭu: ceykiṉṟa (ceykiṉṟu+a)-ceykiṟa.
um - e. kā. īṟu. e-ṭu: ceyyum (cey+um).
u - um (muṉmic cuṭṭaṭiccol)
kuṟippup peyareccam
a (mukkālappotu), e ṭu: nalla.
iṟantakāla nikaḻkālak kuṟippuppeyarecca vīṟākiya a anta eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum cēymiccuṭṭākum. ceytu, ceykiṉṟu, nal  eṉpaṉa muṟkālattil muṟṟuccoṟkaḷāyum irutiṇiyimpāl mūviṭap potuvāyum iruntatiṉāl, viṉiyālaṇiyum peyarāki
ceyta eṉpatu, ceytēṉākiya anta, ceytēmākiya anta, ceytāyākiya anta, ceytīrākiya anta, ceytāṉākiya anta, ceytāḷākiya anta, ceytārākiya anta, ceytatākiya anta, ceytaṉavākiya anta eṉṟu poruḷpaṭṭirukkum.
iṅṅaṉamē ēṉi yirucoṟkaṭkum oṭṭuka.
ceyyum eṉṉum peyareccam, 6ām vēṟṟumip peyar pōla muṉpiṉṉāka māṟiya ceyyuḷ eṉṉum etirkāla viṉi muṟṟāyirukkalām.

ṭu, ṟu eṉpaṉa tuvvīṟṟiṉ puṇarccit tiripeṉpatu muṉṉarē kūṟappaṭṭatu.
ceyteṉa = ceytāṉeṉṟu collumpaṭi, ceytapiṉ, ceytatiṉāl.
ip poruḷi ēṉip pāleṇ ṇiṭaṅkaṭkum oṭṭuka.

	naṉṟu = naṉṟāy	naṉṟu = nallatu.
pu, ā, ū eṉpavaṟṟiṉ mūlamum poruḷum viḷaṅkavilli. ivi ceytu, ōṭi eṉpavaṟṟiṟkup piṟpaṭṭaviyākum.
ā eṉpatu vaṭatiraviṭa vaḻippaṭṭa intiyil, iṟantakāla viṉimuṟṟīṟākavum viṉiyeccavīṟākavum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. ceytu eṉpatu mutaṟkālattil viṉimuṟṟākavum iruntatiṉāl, āvīṟum tamiḻil aṅṅaṉam iruntirukkalām.
nikaḻkāla viṉiyecca vīṟu
ceyya eṉṉum vāypāṭṭuc collē, nikaḻkāla viṉi yeccamākap paṇṭit tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlka ḷellāvaṟṟilum tavaṟākak kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atu āṅkilattil Infinitive Mood eṉṟu collappeṟum etirkālaviṉiyeccamēyaṉṟi vēṟaṉṟu.
uriyum ilakkaṇamum uṭpaṭa, paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiya mellām, potuvāyiṉum ciṟappāyiṉum, ceyyuḷvaṭivilēyē iruntaṉa. ataṉāl, paṇṭit tamiḻilakkaṇaṅkaḷum ceyyuḷ naṭikkē eḻutappeṟṟaṉa. pala coṟkaḷum colvaṭivukaḷum ceyyuḷ naṭiyil iṭampeṟuvatilli. muṉṉūl naṭiyiyē piṉṉūlkaḷum marapākap pōṟṟivantaṉa. āriyar teṉṉāṭu vantu tamiḻ kaṟṟut tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlāciriyarumāṉatiṉāl, tam aṟiyā miyāl kālṭuvelārippōṉṟē pala tavaṟukaḷ ceytuḷḷaṉar. kiṉṟu eṉṉum nikaḻkāla viṉivaṭivam, eṅṅaṉamō iṭik kaḻakattiṟkuppiṉ ceyyuḷil iṭampeṟātu pōyiṟṟu. ataṉāl, ceyyum eṉṉum etirkāla viṉiyiyē nikaḻkālattiṟkum pulavar payaṉpaṭutti vantirukkiṉṟaṉar. āyiṉum, paṇṭi nikaḻ kāla viṉiyiṉ eñcukuṟikaḷ, ilakkiyattilum ilakkaṇat tilum toṭarntu iruntē vantirukkiṉṟaṉa. 
kiṉṟu iṭinilikoṇṭa viṉiyālaṇiyum peyariṉ marūukkaḷ, tolkāppiyattil āṅkāṅkut taṉippaṭa varuvatu ṭaṉ, orē nūṟpāviṟ pala oruṅkukūṭiyum varukiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu:
puṇariyal niliyiṭip poruṇilik kutanavum
 viṉiceyal maruṅkiṟ kālamoṭu varunavum
 vēṟṟumip poruḷvayiṉ urupā kunavum
 acinilik kiḷavi yāki varunavum
 iciniṟik kiḷavi yāki varunavum
 tattam kuṟippiṟ poruḷcey kunavum
 oppil vaḻiyāṟ poruḷcey kunavum		(tol. 735)
utana eṉpatu utavuna eṉpataṉ tokuttal. utavukiṉṟa-utavukuṉṉa-utavukuna-utavuna. ceykiṉṟa-ceykuṉṉa-ceykuna. utavukiṉṟa=utavukiṉṟavi.
porukiṉṟāṉ-porukuṉṉāṉ-poruṉṉāṉ-porunaṉ.
ceyyiṉ allatu ceytāl eṉṉum vāypāṭṭeccam  nilippāṭṭu viṉiyeccamātaliṉ (Subjunctive or Conditional Mood), cariyāṉa etirkāla viṉiyeccamākātu. ilakkaṇa muṟippaṭṭa mukkāla viṉiyecca vāypāṭu kīḻkkāṇum muṟiyilēyē iruttal kūṭum.
i.kā.            	ni.kā.              	e.kā.
ceytu           	ceytukoṇṭu    	ceyya
(Past Participle)  	(Present Participle) 	(Futute Participle or Infinitive Mood)
ceytukoṇṭu eṉṟu tamiḻilum. cēcikkoṉi eṉṟu teluṅkilum, nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccam ulakavaḻakkil toṉṟu toṭṭu vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟatu. ceytukoṇṭu eṉṉum collē yaṉṟi, ceyya eṉṉum col nikaḻkālatti uṇarttātu. ceytukoṇṭu irukkiṟāṉ, ceyya irukkiṟāṉ eṉṉum iru toṭariṉ poruṉiyum ōppunōkki uṇmi teḷika.
ceyya eṉṉum col iyalpāka etirkālattiṟkē uriyatēṉum, muṉṉikaḻcci, oruṅku nikaḻcci, piṉṉikaḻcci eṉṉum mukkāla niliyiyum uṇarttumāṟu āḷappeṟum.
e-ṭu:
maḻipeyyak kuḷam niṟintatu - muṉṉikaḻcci
maṇiyaṭikkak kaḻuti kattiṟṟu - oruṅku nikaḻcci
payirviḷiya maḻipeytatu - piṉṉikaḻcci (e.kā.)
ivaṟṟuḷ, muṉṉikaḻcci piṉvarun toṭarālum, oruṅku nikaḻcci āṭciyiṉālumē aṟiyappaṭum. ceytu eṉṉum coṟ pōl, ceyya eṉṉum col taṉiniṉṟu iṟantakālatti yuṇart tātu. aṅṅaṉamē, ceytukoṇṭu eṉṉum coṟpōl, atu taṉi niṉṟu nikaḻkālatti yuṇarttātu. mēlum, nikaḻkāla nikaḻcci vēṟu; oruṅku nikaḻcci vēṟu. āyiṉum, nīril āḻpavaṉ ciṟu kōliyum paṟṟuvatupōl, ilakkaṇiyar oruṅku nikaḻcciyip paṟṟukkōṭākak koṇṭu, ceyya eṉṉum colli nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccamākak kāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. āyiṉ, ataṟku nikaḻkāla muṇarttum āṟṟaliṉmiyiṉ, muvakit toṭarccikkālatti yum (Continuous Tenses), muṟiyē, 7ām vēṟṟumit toḻiṟpeyarā lum iṟantakāla viṉiyeccattālum muṟṟeccattālumē uṇarttivantirukkiṉṟaṉar.
e-ṭu:
taṭṭuppuṭikkaṇ vantāṉ 	(tol.col.77, iḷam. uri)
- i.kā.toṭarcci
kaṇkava rōviyaṅ kaṇṭuniṟ kunarum  	(maṇimē. 3:131)
- ni.kā.toṭarcci
nīrvārttuk kālkaḻuvā niṉṟu   	(naḷa. 232)        
āṭiṉir pāṭiṉir celiṉē   	(puṟam.109)
- e.kā.toṭarcci
ceytukoṇṭu eṉṉum nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccam (Present Participle) ulakavaḻakkil irukkavum, atip payaṉpaṭuttātu iṅṅaṉam iṭarppaṭuvatu, kaṉiyirukkap pūmpiñcik kavarva toppatē.
paṇṭi yilakkiyattil illāta collellām piṟkālat tavi yeṉak koḷvatu peruntavaṟām. paṇṭi nūlkaḷ iṟṟi akaramutalikaḷalla. potumakkaḷ paḻañcoṟṟokuti talik kaḻakak kālattiṉiṉṟu caṟṟum māṟātu iruntuvantirukkiṉṟatu. āriya varukikku muntiya tamiḻnūl aṉittum iṟantupōyiṉa.
ceytukoṇṭu eṉṉum col, tamiḻiṉ aṭippaṭic coṟkaḷuḷ oṉṟāyum, nāṭṭuppuṟa makkaḷ pēccil āḻa vērūṉṟiya tāyum, tamiḻnāṭeṅkaṇum iṉṟum ceytukiṭṭu, ceytukiṇṭu, ceytukiṉu eṉap palvēṟu koccivaṭivil vaḻaṅkuvatāyum, tamiḻiṉiṉṟu nīkkamuṭiyātatāyum uḷḷatu.
kāṭikkaṇṇi, kutirivāli eṉṉum ciṟu tavacaṅkaḷ toṉṟu toṭṭup pāṇṭināṭṭil viḷintuvariṉum, avi iṉṟuḷḷa paṇṭi yilakkiyattil iṭampeṟavē yilli. iṅṅaṉamē ceytu koṇṭu eṉṉum nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccamum eṉka.
āṅkila nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccattiṉ īṟākiya ing eṉpataṉ tolvaṭivākac collappeṟum inde eṉpatum, ceytiṇṭu eṉṉum koccivaṭivīṟṟi ottiruppatu peritum kavaṉikkat takkatām .
ceyya eṉṉum vāypāṭṭeccam nikaḻkālac collaṉ mayā lēyē, akaravīṟṟu viṉiyeccam eṉa in nūlil itukāṟum kuṟik kappaṭṭatu. iṉi etirkāla viṉiyeccam eṉṟē teḷi vāyk kuṟikkappeṟum.
etirkāla viṉiyecca īṟu:
a. e-ṭu: ceyya, koṭukka, iṟappa.
ceyya eṉpatu ceyyal eṉṉum allīṟṟut toḻiṟ peyariṉ īṟu kēṭām.
ceyyal (ceyal) vēṇṭum = ceyyavēṇṭum.
koṭukkal vēṇṭum = koṭukkavēṇṭum.

iṟakka, ciṟakka, viḻikka, kaḻikka mutaliyavaṟṟi uyarnaṭi yāḷar iṉṉōci karuti iṟappa, ciṟappa, viḻippa, kaḻippa eṉat tirippar.

nilippāṭṭu viṉiyecca īṟukaḷ
āl       		e-ṭu:   	ceytāl (ceytu+āl).
il       		e-ṭu:   	varil.
il-iṉ	e-ṭu:   	ceyyiṉ-ceyiṉ.
kāl    		e-ṭu:   	ceytakkāl.
il eṉpatu iṭapporuḷ allatu karuvipporuḷurupu. varil eṉpatu varukiyil allatu varukiyiṉāl eṉṟu poruḷ paṭuvatu. ātalāl, atu viṉimutaṉiliyellām toḻiṟ peyarāy āḷappaṭṭa kālattut tōṉṟiyatākum. varukiyil = varukiyiṉ pēril.
l-ṉ. varil-variṉ.

iciṉ eṉṉum iṭinili
iciṉ eṉpatu ōr iṟantakāla iṭinili eṉṟu caṅkara namaccivāyarum (naṉ. 145, uri), ōr aciniliyeṉṟu nacciṉārk kiṉiyarum (tol.col. 296) urittaṉar.
iciṉ eṉpatu viṉiyuṟuppāka varum iṭamellām, atu iṟanta kāla viṉiyeccattōṭu cērntē yirukkiṉṟatu. iciṉ eṉpatik koṇṭa viṉikaḷellām iṟantakāla muṟṟākavē yiruppiṉum, paṭarkki viṉimuṟṟukaḷē perumpālum pālīṟu koṇṭaṉavākavum, ēṉi yīriṭa muṟṟukaḷum pālīṟaṟṟaṉavāka vumē yirukkiṉṟaṉa.
iṟantakāla viṭinilikaḷellām iṟantakāla viṉi yeccattuḷḷēyē aṭaṅkiniṟṟaliṉ. ataṉoṭu cērntuḷḷa iciṉ eṉpatu oru tuṇiviṉiyākavē yiruttalvēṇṭum. atu ī eṉpatē. atu utavi viṉiyīṟukaḷuḷ oṉṟeṉpatu muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭatu.
āyiṉōr, pōyiṉōr, mēyiṉōr, tāyiṉōr eṉpaṉa pōṉṟu, īyiṉōr eṉpatum paṭarkkip palarpāl iṟantakāla viṉiyālaṇiyum peyarām. yakara cakarap pōliyil atu īciṉōr eṉṟāki iciṉōr eṉṟu kuṟukum. piṉpu atu iṟantakāla viṉiyeccattuṭaṉ tuṇiviṉiyākac cērntu,

eṉṉum tolkāppiya uriccol iyalviḷakka nūṟpāvum, tolkāppiyar uriccoṟkaṭku akaramutali muṟiyiṟ poruḷē kūṟic celvatum, uriccoṟkut taṉiyilakkaṇavakit taṉmi yiṉmiyum,

(5) aivakic coṉṉili
1.	acinili (Isolating or Monosyllabic Stage)
	e-ṭu: il, āḷ.
2.	puṇarnili (Compounding Stage)
	e-ṭu: il-āḷ.
3.	pakucoṉṉili allatu īṟupēṟṟa nili (Inflexional Stage)
	e-ṭu: illāḷ.
4.	koḷuvunili (Agglutinative Stage)
	e-ṭu: ceyvippi.
5.	tokinili (Synthetic Stage)
	e-ṭu: maka+kaḷ=makkaḷ, tam+āy=tāy, ātaṉ+tā=āntā-ānti.

(7) piṉṉamippu (Back formation)
oru tiricolliṉ mutali allatu īṟṟi nīkki maṟṟoru colli amittukkoḷvatu piṉṉamippām.
e-ṭu: 
muḻuttal = tiraḷutal, paruttal. muḻu-muḻā = tiraṇṭamuracu.
muḻā-miḻā = parutta māṉvaki (stag). miḻā-mēḻam-			mēḻakam-ēḻakam-ēṭakam-ēṭu-yāṭu-āṭu.
mēḻam = parutta cemmaṟiyāṭṭuk kaṭā. mēḻakam  = 			cemmaṟikkaṭā.
vempari mēḻaka mēṟṟi				(cīvaka.521)
ēḻakam = cemmaṟikkaṭā, cemmaṟi, veḷḷāṭu.
yāṭu = āṭṭiṉpotu 		(tol: poruḷ.567)
mēḻakam eṉṉum colliṉ tiripiṉiṉṟu āṭu eṉṉum colli amittukkoṇṭatu piṉṉamippām.
vēntaṉ eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu vēntu eṉṉum vaṭivi amittatum piṉṉamippē. vēyntōṉ = muṭiyaṇintōṉ.
vēyntōṉ-vēntaṉ = muṭiyaṇinta cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyaruḷ oruvaṉ. vēntaṉ-vēntu.
(8) coṟpaṇpaṭuttam
1.moḻimutalākā mutaleḻutti moḻimutaleḻuttākkal.
e-ṭu: 
lālāṭṭu-rārāṭṭu-rōrāṭṭu.
eṉpatu paṇpaṭāta ulakavaḻakku. rōrō eṉṟu āṭṭuvatu rōrāṭṭu.
tālāṭṭu, ōlāṭṭu-ōrāṭṭu eṉpaṉa paṇpaṭṭa tamiḻ vaḻakku.
la-ta, pōli. o.nō: calaṅki-cataṅki.
loḷuloḷu eṉpati noḷunoḷu eṉpatum, loḷloḷ eṉṟu kulikkum nāyi ñeḷḷi eṉpatum, aḵtē.
ṭakaramutal takaramutalāka eḻutappaṭum.
	e-ṭu: 
ṭavaṇṭi-tavaṇṭi (oruvakip paṟi).
avari, tuvari, paṉi, mali mutaliya coṟkaḷiṉ aikāravīṟu, akaravīṟṟiṉ paṇpaṭuttat tiripāyirukkalām.
2. coṟkaḷip poruṭkēṟpa vēṟupaṭuttal.
	e-ṭu: 
taliyaṉ, talivaṉ, taliccaṉ, taliyāri, talikāraṉ.
kaṭiya, kaṭakka; kaṭital, kaṭittal; kaṭintāṉ, kaṭittāṉ.
kaṭital=nīkkutal. kaṭittal=pallāl veṭṭutal.
veḷku-veṭku, veḷku-veḵku.
veṭkutal=nāṇutal,veḵkutal=piṟarporuḷi 				virumputal.
nīrniliyik kuṟikkum āṟu eṉṉum colli vēṟ ṟumip puṇarcciyil valiyiraṭṭittalum, eṇṇik kuṟikkum āṟu eṉṉum colli aṅṅaṉam iraṭṭikkāmiyum, paṇ paṭuttattiṉ pāṟpaṭṭatē.
tiripu puṇarcciyāl makaṉmi kuṟittal
kīraṉ+koṟṟaṉ=kīraṅkoṟṟaṉ (kīraṉ makaṉākiya koṟṟaṉ)
kaṇṇaṉ+cēntaṉār=kaṇṇañcēntaṉār (kaṇṇaṉ 			makaṉākiya cēntaṉār)
piṭṭaṉ+tattaṉ=piṭṭantattaṉ (piṭṭaṉ makaṉākiya tattaṉ)
ivaṟṟil, nilimoḻiyīṟṟu ṉakaramey, varumoḻimutal valliṉa meykku iṉamelliṉamāyt tirital kāṇka. vaṭukañ cāttaṉ eṉpatu vaṭukaccāttaṉ eṉṟu valippiṉ, vaṭukaṉ makaṉākiya cāttaṉ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭātu, vaṭukaṉ (teluṅkaṉ) ākiya cāttaṉ eṉṟē poruḷpaṭum.
(9) coṟṟūymi
tamiḻ tāṉē tōṉṟiya peruvaḷat tāymoḻiyātaliṉ, coṟṟūymi ataṉ ciṟanta paṇpukaḷuḷ oṉṟām. ataṉāl, paṇṭit tamiḻar atik kaṇṇuṅkaruttumāyp pēṇi, ayal nāṭṭiliruntu vanta poruḷkaṭkellām uṭaṉuṭaṉ tūya tamiḻccoṟ peyarkaḷiyē puṉintiṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar.
	e-ṭu:
poruḷ tōṉṟiya iṭam	poruṭpeyar
arapi                               	kutiri, pērīntu
cīṉam                               	karumpu
amerikkā, pāracīkam	pukiyili
amerikkā                       	uruḷikkiḻaṅku
mēliyintiyat tīvukaḷ          	aṇṭimā, KªâÇ(Cashew)
turukki                            	vāṉkōḻi
teṉṉamerikkā,                	centāḻi (Pine apple)
mēliyintiyat tīvukaḷ     
iṅkilāntu                       	pukivaṇṭi
	mitivaṇṭi (cycle)
kaḵṭu, kaḵtu, kaḵpu eṉṉum coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷ yāviyeṉṟē teriyātu muṟṟum maṟintuviṭṭaṉa. iṭā, oruñār, oru tuvali eṉpaṉa evvaki aḷaveṉṟu teriyavilli.
oṭṭakamum kutiripōl arapināṭṭiṉiṉṟu vantatē. tolkāppiya marapiyalil oṭṭakam kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. tamiḻnāṭu arapināṭṭoṭu toṉṟutoṭṭu vaṇikam ceytuvantatiṉāl, oṭṭakam tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku vantatoṭu ataṉ peyarum ilakkiyat til maṭṭumaṉṟi ilakkaṇattilum iṭampeṟalāyiṟṟu. ataṉ arapip peyar camal (Jamal) eṉpatām. ākavē, oṭṭakam eṉpatu akkāla marapuppaṭi tamiḻccollākavē yiruttal vēṇṭum. oru mātamvari paṭṭiṉiyirukkum tiṟam oṭṭakattiṉ ciṟappiyalpām. oṭṭap pōṭutal = paṭṭiṉiyiruttal. ātalāl, at tiṟampaṟṟi ataṟku ap peyar iṭappaṭṭirukkalām. camaṟkirutattil ari uṣṭrakka eṉṟu tirittu, atiṉiṉṟu oṭṭakam eṉṉum col vantatākak kūṟuvar. centamiḻic cīrkulikkum ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻakaramutaliyilum, iṅṅaṉamē kuṟikkappaṭ ṭuḷatu. erital viṉi yik kuṟikkum uṣ eṉṉum aṭiyiṉiṉṟu uṣṭrakka eṉṉum peyar tōṉṟiyatākak kūṟuvar vaṭavar. itu poruntap poyttal eṉṉum uttiyē. āriyar intiyāviṟkuṭ pukumuṉṉarē oṭṭakam tamiḻakattiṟkuṭ pukuntu viṭṭatu. mēlum, uṣ eṉṉum vaṭacollum uḷ (oḷ) eṉṉum teṉcolliṉ tiriyē.
oṭṭakattiṟku neṭuṅkaḻuttaṉ-neṭuṅkaḻuttal, neṭuṅ kōṇi eṉṟum peyaruṇṭu.
āriyac cārpiṉāl, tamiḻ keṭṭatumaṉṟit tamiḻar tam tāymoḻi yuṇarcciyiyum iḻantaṉar. ataṉāl avarum tāḻvuṟṟaṉar. avar mīṇṭum taḻikkavēṇṭiṉ, tam muṉṉō rippōl tamiḻip pōṟṟalvēṇṭum. ākavē, mōṭṭār eṉpati iyaṅki eṉṟum, kāppi eṉpatik kuḷampi eṉṟum, tēnīr eṉpatik koḻuntunīr eṉṟum, colvatē takkatām.
piṟamoḻic collik kaṭaṉkoḷvatāl tamiḻ vaḷarum eṉpār, tamiḻaṟiyātavarum tamiḻppakivarum tamiḻik kāṭṭik
koṭuppavarumē yāvar.
(10) colvaḷam
tamiḻ mikunta colvaḷamuṭiyateṉpati, nālvaki yilip peyarālum, aivakip pūnilip peyarālum, mukkaṉi kaḷiṉ piñcu nilip peyarālum, vevvēṟu ciṟappup poruḷiṟ collutalik kuṟikkum aṟi, iyampu, ici, uri, eṉ, ōtu, kiḷa, kiḷattu, kūṟu, cāṟṟu, ceppu, col, navil, nutal, nuval, noṭi, pakar, paṟi, paṉṉu, paṉuvu, pukal, pulampu, pēcu, māṟu, miḻaṟṟu, moḻi, viḷattu, viḷampu mutaliya coṟkaḷālum, eḻunilip peṇṇiḷamip peyarkaḷālum, piṟavaṟṟālum aṟiyalām.
veḷināṭṭiṉiṉṟu vanta kutiriyiṉattikkūṭa, eṇvaki yākavum tamiḻar vakuttu, avaṟṟiṟkēṟpap peyariṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar.
mūvēntar kutirikaḷum kuṟunilamaṉṉar kutiriyum vev vēṟiṉattic cērntavi, pāṇṭiyaṉ kutiri kaṉavaṭṭam; cōḻaṉ kutiri kōram. ivi eṇvakiyuḷ aṭaṅkātavi. ivaṟṟiṉ viḷakkatti eṉ paṇṭittamiḻ nākarikamum paṇpāṭum eṉṉum nūliṟ kāṇka.

tavacamaṇipaṟṟiya coṟkaḷ
muṟṟa viḷintatu maṇi; ariviḷiccalāṉatu ari vayiṟaṉ; uḷḷīṭaṟṟatu pokku allatu patar.
cottit tēṅkāypaṟṟiya coṟkaḷ
kōṭṭāṉ uṭkārntatu kōṭṭāṉkāy allatu kūkik kāy; tēri amarntatu tērikkāy; oṟṟiyāḷ teṉṉi marattaṭiyil iḷanīr kuṭittatāl ēṟpaṭṭatu ollittēṅkāy; muṟkūṟiya iruvakiyum allittēṅkāy. ōṟṟiyāḷ kuṭittāl ollipaṭum eṉpatu colavaṭi. itu, pakkattiliruppavariyum kuṭippiyātu taṉiyāyk kuṭittal kūṭāteṉṉum tamiḻap paṇpāṭṭi uṇarttuvatākum.
ōṇāṉ vakipaṟṟiya coṟkaḷ
cillāṉ, ōṇāṉ, karaṭṭi, kōmpi (paccōnti).
piṇampaṟṟiya coṟkaḷ 
cettaṇimiyāṉatu cavam; iṟantu oru nāṭku mēṟpaṭṭuk kaṭṭuviṭṭatu piṇam; paṉṉāḷāki aḻukippōṉatu aḻaṉ.
iṅṅaṉam collikkoṇṭē pōkiṉ oru perunūlāy viriyumātaliṉ, immaṭṭil iṅku niṟuttalāyiṟṟu. iṟṟi niliyilēyē oruporuṭ palacoṟkaḷ tamiḻil ērāḷamā yuḷḷaṉa. paṇṭi yilakkiyamellām ceyyuḷiliruntatiṉāl, mōṉi yetukik kutavumāṟu orē uyirikkup pala peyarkaḷi amit tirukkiṉṟaṉar.
e-ṭu: 	yāṉi-āmpal, umpal, uvā, eṟumpi, ōṅkal, kaṭamā, kaṭivi, kampamā, kari, kavaḷamā, kaḷiṟu, kaṟiyaṭi, kuñcaram, kumpi, kippuli, kimmali, kimmā, cinturam, tumpi, tūṅkal, tōl, nālvāy, pakaṭu, piṇimukam, pukarmukam, puḻikki-pūṭki, perumā, poṅkaṭi, matamā, mataṅkam, matāvaḷam, maruṇmā, moy, vaḻuvi, vāraṇam, vēḻam.
iṅṅaṉam vēṟem moḻiyilum kāṇṭaṟkaritu . vaṭamoḻi yiliruppavi, perumpālum tamiḻiliruntu kaṭaṉkoṇṭaṉavum tamiḻccoṟkaḷiṉ moḻipeyarppumē.
īrāyiraṅ kal teṟku nīṇṭirunta paḻampāṇṭināṭ ṭulakavaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum, mutaliru kaḻakattup  pallāyiram taṉittamiḻ nūlkaḷiṉ ilakkiyaccoṟkaḷum, iṉṟiruntiruppiṉ,  tamiḻc colvaḷam ettuṇip parantupaṭṭirukkum eṉpati uyt tuṇarntu koḷka.
(11) moḻiccemmi
āriyar tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku vantapiṉ, aṟiyāmiyālum cōmpalālum tāḻvuṇarcciyālum tamiḻppaṟṟiṉmiyālum ēṟpaṭṭa koccivaḻakku, nīṇṭakālamāka iruntutāṉ vantirukkiṉṟatu.  āyiṉum, ēṭeṭut teḻutumpōtum mēṭi yēṟip pēcumpōtum tiruntiya naṭiyiyē kiyāḷavēṇṭu meṉṟu, toṉṉūlāciriyar ōr aḻiyā varampiṭṭuviṭṭaṉar. av varampē, puravalarum pulavarum illākkālattum, pakivarum koṇṭāṉmārum palkiyapōtum, pintamiḻi vēliyākak kāttu vantirukkiṉṟatu. iv varampu ēṉi moḻikaṭkilli. ataṉāl, avi makkaḷ vāykku vanta vaki yellām vaḻaṅki, avaṟṟiṉ pala oḻuṅkiṉṟiyum urutteriyātum pōyiṉa. tamiḻukku etu vaḻuniliyō atu piṟamoḻikaṭku vaḻāniliyāyiṟṟu.
ceruppu, tiruppu, neruppu, paruppu eṉpaṉa, muṟiyē ceppu, tippu, nippu, pappu eṉat tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiṉ vaḻuniliyām; teluṅkil vaḻaṅkiṉ vaḻāniliyām.
ikara aikāravīṟṟup peyarkaḷ 4ām vēṟṟumiyēṟkum pōtu, kuvvurupu kiyyākat tirivatu tamiḻukku vaḻuniliyām; teluṅku mutaliya piṟamoḻikaṭku vaḻāniliyām.
e-ṭu:	kiḷikki, malikki - tamiḻ (vaḻunili)
       	pulikki, akkaṭikki - teluṅku (vaḻānili)
ammi, kari, kuṭi, paṉi, maḻi mutaliya aikāravīṟṟuc coṟkaḷi akaravīṟāka olippatum, ceyyavēṇṭum eṉpatic ceyyēṇam eṉṟu colvatum, tamiḻukku vaḻuniliyām; maliyāḷattiṟku vaḻāniliyām.
coṟkaḷiṉ īṟṟil varum melliṉa meykaḷ, muyaṟciyoṭu palukkappeṟātu mūkkoliyaḷavāy niṉṟuviṭiṉ, tamiḻukku vaḻuniliyām; marātti, inti mutaliya moḻikaṭku vaḻānili yām.
ḻakaratti lakaramākavum, ākāratti īkāra ēkāraṅkaṭ kiṭippaṭṭa oliyākavum, olippatu tamiḻukku vaḻuniliyām; āṅkilattiṟku vaḻāniliyām.
coṟkaḷ tam ciṟappupporu ḷiḻantum vaṭivu citintum vaḻaṅkuvatu, tamiḻukku vaḻuniliyām; piṟa moḻikaṭkellām vaḻā niliyām.
pāṇṭināṭṭut tamiḻic centamiḻ eṉṟum, piṟanāṭṭut tami ḻik koṭuntamiḻ eṉṟum, paṇṭikkālattil pirittu vaḻaṅkiyatu, avaṟṟiṟkuc cemmiyeṉṉum paṇpu uṇmi yiṉmipaṟṟiyē. ikkālattuc cila koṇṭāṉmār talimit tamiḻp pērāciriyap patavi peṟṟu, mēṉāṭṭārip piṉpaṟṟi, potumakkaḷ pēcuvatē moḻiyeṉṟu, piṟamoḻikaḷiṉ vaḻuniliyit tamiḻiṟ pukutti, ataṉ cemmiyaraṇit takarkkap pārkkiṉṟaṉar. nakkīraṉmār avarik kaṭuttut tiruttutal vaṇṭum. ulaka moḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟuḷḷum, cemmi eṉṉum varampuḷḷatu tamiḻ oṉṟē.
(12) marapuvaḻakku
marapāvatu iḷamippeyar, āṇpāṟpeyar, peṇpāṟ peyar, nilittiṇic ciṉippeyar, vilaṅkuk kāvalar peyar, aḵṟiṇi yuyirikaḷiṉ kattalviṉikaḷ, ūṇviṉikaḷ mutaliyaṉapaṟṟi, uyarntōr toṉṟutoṭṭu ec coṟkaḷi vaḻaṅkiṉārkaḷō ac coṟkaḷiyē vaḻaṅkutal.
potuvākap paṟavikaḷiṉ iḷamiyik kuñcu eṉṟum, vilaṅkukaḷiṉ iḷamiyik kuṭṭi eṉṟum collavēṇṭum. ūruyirikaḷiṉ iḷamippeyar iv viraṇṭil oṉṟāyirukkum. naṇṭukkuñcu eṉṟum elikkuṭṭi eṉṟum kūṟuvatu marapu. āyiṉum, elikkuṭṭiyi elikkuñcu eṉṉum vaḻakkumuṇṭu. āyiṉ, naṇṭukkuñci naṇṭukkuṭṭiyeṉṉum vaḻakkam illavē yilli. makkaḷ iḷamiyi makavu, kuḻavi, piḷḷi eṉṟu colvatē marapāyiṉum, kaṭicippiḷḷiyik kaṭikkuṭṭi eṉṉum vaḻakkuṇṭu.
piḷḷi eṉṉum iḷamippeyar ēṟattāḻa ellā vuyiriṉattilum ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkum. irukkum piḷḷi mūṉṟu, ōṭum piḷḷi mūṉṟu, paṟakkumpiḷḷi mūṉṟu eṉa oru colavaṭiyuṇṭu. āyiṉ, iv varampiṟantu piḷḷiccol vaḻaṅkuvatu tavaṟām.
kiḻavaṉ eṉṉum col peṇpāliṟ kiḻatti eṉṟāvatu pōl pulavaṉ eṉṉum col pulatti eṉṟākum. iṅṅaṉam amikkāta peṇpāṟpulavar eṉpatu vaḻuvām.
taṉ collit tavaṟāy vaḻaṅkuvatupōṉṟē, ayaṟ colli āḷvatum marapuvaḻuvām. vālipaṉ, vālipappeṇ eṉpavaṟṟiṟkup pakaramāka, iḷiñaṉ, iḷiñi eṉṉum teṉcoṟkaḷiyē vaḻaṅkavēṇṭum.
veṅkāyappal, palāccuḷi, paṉinuṅku, vāḻippaḻac cati, kaṟṟāḻañcōṟu eṉṟu kūṟuvatu marapām.
āṭṭiṭiyaṉ, paṉṟimēyppaṉ, kutirivātuvaṉ, yāṉip pākaṉ eṉṟē kūṟutalvēṇṭum.
kākam karikiṟatu, kuyil kūvukiṟatu, mayil akavukiṟatu, ānti kiḷi kūṭṭukiṟatu, kūki kuḻaṟukiṟatu, tavaḷi paṟiyaṭik kiṟatu, palli muṟkanteṟikkiṟatu. kutiri kaṉikkiṟatu, yāṉi piḷiṟukiṟatu, puli uṟumukiṟatu, arimā uraṟukiṟatu eṉpaṉa kattal viṉi marapākum.
cōṟu cāppiṭutal, kuḷampi(kāppi) kuṭittal, palakāram tiṉṉu tal eṉpatē marapu. kuḷampi cāppiṭutal eṉpatu marapu vaḻuvām.
ip poli oru kōṭṭi kāṇum, im maruntu kātu valiyik kēṭkum, eṉ vaṇṭi paḻutupaṭṭu nūṟurupāvik kēṭṭuviṭṭatu, putuvīṭu oru periya urupāvi viḻuṅkiviṭṭatu. ivi pōṉṟaṉavum marapuvaḻakkē.
(13) coṟṟoṭar vakikaḷ
paṇṭic tamiḻilakkiya mellām ceyyuḷ naṭiyilirunta miyālum, ilakkaṇamellām ceyyuḷmoḻikkē eḻutap paṭṭa miyālum, urinaṭikkuc ciṟappāṉa ilakkaṇak kūṟukaḷip paḻantamiḻilakkaṇa nulkaḷ eṭuttukkūṟavilli.
coṟṟoṭarvakiyiṟ paṇṭit tamiḻilakkaṇaṅkaḷ kūṟiyavellām, aṟuvakit tokinilittoṭarum eṇ vakit tokānilit toṭarumē. avaṟṟuḷ, eḻuvāyt toṭarum viḷit toṭarum viṉimuṟṟuttoṭarum toṭariyam allatu muṟṟuc coṟṟoṭarākum. āyiṉ, avi irucoṟ ṟoṭarāṉa taṉit toṭariyamē.
urinaṭikku ilakkaṇam eḻutappaṭāviṭiṉum, coṟ ṟoṭaramippu iṉṟupōṉṟē aṉṟum ulakavaḻakku  moḻiyil iruntatu. ataṉāl, taṉit toṭariyam (simple sentence,) kūṭṭut toṭariyam (compound  sentence), kalapput toṭariyam (complex sentence), kalavi allatu katampat toṭariyam (mixed sentence) eṉṉum toṭariya vaki nāṉkum; nēr kūṟṟu (direct speech) nēralkūṟṟu (indirect Speech) eṉṉum kūṟṟuvaki yiraṇṭum; ilakkiyan tōṉṟumuṉṉarē tamiḻil amintiruttal vēṇṭum.
āṅkilattiṟkum tamiḻiṟkum palavakiyil oppumi yiruppatāl, ituvari aṟiyappaṭāta cila tamiḻ urinaṭi yilakkaṇak kūṟukaḷi āṅkilattiṉiṉṟē aṟiya muṭikiṉṟatu.
(1) ceyya eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉiyeccam
iv viṉiyeccam allīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyariṉiṉṟē tōṉṟiyiruppatāl, āṅkilattiṟpōl tamiḻilum eḻuvāy (Noun Infinitive) ātal kūṭum.
e-ṭu: 	eṉakkup pāṭatteriyum = eṉakkup pāṭal (pāṭum 	viṉi) teriyum.
itiṟ pāṭṭu eṉṉum peyar tokkuniṉṟatākak koḷḷa muṭiyātu. eṉakkup pāṭṭuppāṭat teriyum eṉṟu it toṭari yatti virippiṉ, eṉakku varatteriyum eṉpatu, varavuvarat teriyum eṉṟu virittaṟ kiṭantarāmi kāṇka.
(2) ceytu eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉiyeccam
kōḻi kūvip poḻutu viṭintatu.
itiṟ kūvi eṉpatik kūva eṉat tirippar tamiḻ ilak kaṇiyar. āṅkilattiṟpōl taṉinili yamippit (Absolute construction) tamiḻukkuṅ koḷḷiṉ, iṅṅaṉan tirikkat tēviyē yilli. ittakiya amippu tamiḻil arukivarātu peruvaḻakkā yiruttal kavaṉikkattakkatu.
e-ṭu: kūṭṭam muṭintu, ellārum pōyviṭṭārkaḷ.
(3) mukkāla nannālvaki
itu muṉṉarē viṉiccoṟ pakutiyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuviṭṭatu. āṇṭuk kāṇka.
iii poruḷ
tamiḻ ilakkaṇattiṉ mūṉṟām pakuti poruḷ eṉpatākum. colliṟkē poruḷuṇmiyālum, paṇṭiyilakkiyamellām ceyyuḷ vaṭivi liruntamiyālum, ceyyuṭkut toṭariyamaṉṟi eḻuttu aci cīr taḷi aṭi toṭi eṉpaṉavē uṟuppāki yālum, colliṟkaṭutta moḻiyuṟuppi allatu ilakkaṇap pakutiyip poruḷ eṉṟē koṇṭaṉar, mutaṉūlāciriyaṉum vaḻinūlāciri yarumāṉa muṉṉūlāciriyar.
ilakkiyamellām  allatu eḻutappeṟṟaṉavellām ceyyuḷā yiruntamiyāl,  poruḷil yāppum aṭaṅkiṟṟu. vallōr aṇipeṟac ceyvaṉavē ceyyuḷātalāl, yāppil aṇiyum aṭaṅkiṟṟu.
poruḷi akam, puṟam eṉa irupālāy vakuttu av viru pāṟkuḷḷum ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum tam nuṇmāṇ nuḻi pulattāl viyakkattakka muṟiyil aṭakkiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. ip poru ḷilakkaṇamē, paṇṭip pulavariyum pulamimikka iṟivaṉaṭi yāriyum iṉpakkaṭaluḷ āḻttiyatu. kumarik kaṇṭap potumakkaḷ moḻiyamippil tam nuṇṇuṇarvip pulappaṭuttiyiruppatu pōṉṟē, pulamakkaḷum  poruḷilak kaṇattil tam nuṇmatiyic ciṟappak kāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. āyiṉ, iti ikkālap pulavar kātaṟciṟappiyum pōrttiṟattiyumē viḷakkuvatākap piṟaḻa vuṇarntuḷḷaṉar. itaṉ virivāṉa viḷak katti eṉ tolkāppiya viḷakkam eṉṉum nūliṟ kāṇka.
veṇpā, āriciyappā, kalippā, vañcippā eṉṉum taliciṟanta ceyyuḷ vakikaḷik kaṇṭu, yāppilum tamiḻi oppuyarvaṟṟa tākkiyuḷḷaṉar paṇṭit tamiḻppulavar.
māttiri, eḻuttu, aci, cīr, aṭi, yāppu, marapu, tūkku, toṭi, nōkku, pā, aḷavu, tiṇi, kikōḷ, kūṟṟu, kēṭpōr, kaḷaṉ, kālam, payaṉ, meyppāṭu, eccam, muṉṉam, poruḷ, tuṟi, māṭṭu, vaṇṇam eṉṉum irupattāṟu ṟuppukaḷikkoṇṭu; pāṭṭu, uri, nūl, vāymoḻi, pici, aṅkatam, mutucol (paḻamoḻi) eṉṉum eḻunilatteḻunta nālvakic ceyyuḷkaḷālāṉa; ammi, aḻaku, toṉmi, tōl, viruntu, iyipu, pulaṉ, iḻipu eṉṉum eṇvaki vaṉappukaḷi (kāviyaṅkaḷi), ki.mu. 10,000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē, mutaṉmutalāka ulakil iyaṟṟiyavar kumarikkaṇṭat tamiḻppulavar. avar ceyyuḷiṟ pēccuvaṉmiyum peṟṟiruntaṉar. aṟivilum āṟṟalilum avariyottavar ikkālat toruvarumilli. innāḷit taliciṟanta pāvalarum, paṇṭi nallicip pulavari nōkka, pāṇarum pullicip pulavarumē (Bards and Poetasters) - iṟṟit tamiḻp pulavarō kumarikkaṇṭap potumakkaṭkum īṭākār.
ceyyuḷ ciṟanta kalamākavum ataṉ poruḷ ciṟanta amutākavum karutappeṟṟatiṉāl, vēṟemmoḻiyilu millāta poruḷilakkaṇam, tamiḻilakkaṇattiṉ koṭumuṭiyum muṭi maṇiyum mutir viḷivum uyirnāṭiyum tamiḻaṉiṉ taṉipperum perumiyum, ākak koḷḷappeṟṟatu.
7. talikkaḻakam (tōrā- ki.mu. 10,000-5500)
tamiḻ makkaḷ moḻittuṟiyilum ilakkiyattuṟiyilum mika vuyariya niliyaṭintiruntataṉālum, tamiḻvāyilāyk kalviyip parappuvatu aracaṉ kaṭamiyātalālum, pāṇṭiya vēntar cilarum nallicip pulavarāyiruntatiṉālum, kāyciṉa vaḻuti eṉṉum pāṇṭiyaṉ, paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟaṅkariyilirunta maturi eṉṉum taṉ talinakaril, talimip pulavariyellām kūṭṭi oru tamiḻkkaḻakam niṟuviṉāṉ. ak kaḻakappulavar, paḻiya ilakki yatti ārāyvatum putiya nūlkaḷiyum vaṉappukaḷiyum pāṭalkaḷiyum iyaṟṟuvatum, tam toḻilākak koṇṭirun taṉar. putiya iyaṟṟalkaḷellām pāṇṭiyaṉ muṉṉiliyil araṅkēṟṟappeṟṟē āṭcikku vantaṉa.
aṟṟit tamiḻakattil āriyarumilli; āriyac collō karuttō kalanta nūlumilli.
iṉṉicikkaliyum nāṭakakkaliyum nalvaḷarcci yaṭintiruntamiyāl, moḻiyoṭu icikkum naṭippiṟkumuḷḷa neruṅkiya toṭarpu karutiyum, icippāṭṭukaḷellām ceyyuḷ vakiyāyiruttal paṟṟiyum, iciyum nāṭakamum moḻiyoṭu cērkkappeṟṟu, iyalici nāṭakameṉat tamiḻ muttamiḻāy vaḻaṅkivaralāyiṟṟu. iyaṟṟamiḻi maṭṭum kūṟum ilakkaṇam piṇṭam eṉṟum, muttamiḻiyum kūṟum ilakkaṇam māpiṇṭam eṉṟum peyarpeṟṟaṉa.
2
tirinilip paṭalam
(ki.mu. 20,000-iṉṟuvari)
kumarikkaṇṭa māntar, tamiḻiṉ aivakic coṉṉilik kālattilum, makkaṭperukkam, iyaṟki viḷivukkuṟivu, pañcam, koḷḷi, pōr, paki, vēṟṟiṭaviruppu, tuṇiceyal vēṭki, vaṇikam, kaṭalkōḷ mutaliya palvēṟu karaṇiyaṅkaḷāl, kiḻakkum vaṭakiḻakkum vaṭakkum vaṭamēṟkum mēṟkumākak kūṭṭaṅ kūṭṭamāyp pirintupōyiṉar. vaṭakiḻakkuc ceṉṟa turēṉiyar (cittiyar) acinilik kālattilum puṇarnilik kālattilum, vaṭamēṟkiṟ ceṉṟa āriyar pakucoṉṉilik kālattilum pirintupōṉatākat terikiṉṟatu. nēr vaṭakkuc ceṉṟavar tamiḻiṉ muṉṉilik kālattilum piṉṉilik kālattilum pirintavarāta liṉ, avar ceṉṟa viṭamellām, pala vūrppeyarkaḷ iṉṟum tamiḻccollāyum avaṟṟiṉ tiripāyumiruppatuṭaṉ, avar moḻikaḷum tamiḻoṭu ciṟitum peritum toṭarpuṭiyaṉa vāyirukkiṉṟaṉa.
1. ūrppeyarkaḷ
teluṅkanāṭu: ciṟṟūr(cittūr), nellūr, kuṇṭūr, orukal allatu ōrāṅkal (Warangal).
oṭṭaram (oricā): kaṭakam (Cuttack)=tisªj matil, matil cūḻnta nakaram.
pampāy: vēḷūr, vēḷūrakam (Ellora), vēḷāpuram (Velapur), vēḷakam, ntŸ(»uhk«)-(Belgum), vēḷpaṭṭi (Belhutti). vēḷā eṉpatu palavūrkaḷiṉ potuppeyarāyuḷḷatu. caḷukkiyar āṇṭa pampāy maṇṭalappakuti vēḷ pulam eṉappaṭṭatu.
kuccaram: tuvāraki (Dwaraka). itu erumiyūr (micūr) nāṭṭi luḷḷa tuvariyiṉ(tuvāracamuttiram) peyarāl aminta nakar. tuvar = civappu, cempu. tuvar- tuvari = ceppuk kōṭṭiyuḷḷa nakar.
cempupuṉin tiyaṟṟiya cēṇeṭum purici
 yuvarā vīkit tuvari yāṇṭu			 (puṟam. 201)
akattiyar tuvarāpatip pōntu nilaṅkaṭanta neṭumuṭi yaṇṇal vaḻikkaṇ aracar patiṉeṇmariyum, patiṉeṇ vēḷi ruḷḷiṭṭāriyum aruvāḷariyuṅ koṇṭu pōntu
eṉṉum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟṟu, tuvāraki, vēḷpulam, aruvānāṭu ākiyavaṟṟiṉ aṇmiyi nōkkumpōtu naṉṟāy viḷaṅkum.
uttaramaṇṭalam: maturi (Muttra). itu talikkaḻaka maturiyiṉ 	peyar koṇṭatu. iv vaṭamaturiyiṟ piṟanta kaṇṇaṉ (kiruṭṭiṇaṉ) eṉṉum aracaṉ iṭiyar kulattiṟ piṟantu tamiḻ iṭiyar pōl ēṟutaḻuvi maṇanta oru tiraviṭa maṉṉaṉē. kiruṣṇa (karuppaṉ, kariyaṉ) eṉṉum peyariṉ kruṣ eṉṉum mutaṉiliyum, karuḷ eṉṉum teṉcol tiripē. kaḷ-karu-karuḷ. karuḷutal karuttal. matupura eṉpatu maturā eṉat tirintateṉpatu poruntātu. 
pīkār: pāṭalipuram (Patna). itu pātirippuliyūr eṉṉum tamiḻppeyariṉ vaṭamoḻippeyarppu. itu pāṭali puttiram eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum. mutalil, teṉṉārkkāṭṭu māvaṭṭattic cērnta tiruppātirippuliyūr pāṭali puttiram eṉappaṭṭatu.  ataṉpiṉ atu vaṭanāṭṭu nakarap peyarāy amintatu. pātiri eṉṉum pūppeyar vaṭa moḻiyiṟ pāṭali eṉat tiriyum.
oṇṇiṟap pātirippūrc cērtalāl		(nālaṭi. 139)
vaṅkāḷam: fhË¡nfh£l«(Calcutta). itu kāḷikōyilāṟ peṟṟa peyar.
kāḷi mutaliṟ pālinilat teyvamāyiruntu, piṉṉarp pōril veṟṟitarupavaḷ (koṟṟavi) eṉṟum, amminōyi uṇṭākku pavaḷum nīkkupavaḷum eṉṟum, nampappaṭṭatiṉāl ēṉi nilaṅkaḷiṉ teyvamāyum, tamiḻnāṭṭil mutaṉmutalāka vaṇaṅkappaṭṭavaḷ. kōyilik kōṭṭam eṉpatu paḻantamiḻ marapu. vētakkāla āriyatteyvaṅkaḷuḷ kāḷi illi. vaṅkañ ceṉṟa piṉṉarē āriyar kāḷi vaṇakkatti mēṟkoṇṭaṉar; kāḷi eṉṉum peyar karuppi eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum tūya teṉ col; kaḷ-kāḷ-kāḷam-kāḷi. mumumutaṟ kaṭavuṭ koḷkiyum uruvavaṇakkamum kōyil vaḻipāṭum vēta āriyarkkilli.
tamluk. itu tamiḻakam eṉpataṉ tiripāyirukkalām. vaṅkañ ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiya paṇṭit tamiḻa vaṇikar ip peyari iṭṭaṉar pōlum!
ūr, puram, puri mutaliya tamiḻiṭappeya rīṟukaḷ, tirintum tiriyātum iṉṟum vaṭanāṭṭiṟ pala iṭaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. mutalil, puram eṉpatu kōpuramuḷḷa nakariyum, puri eṉpatu matil cūḻnta nakariyum kuṟittaṉa.
2. kulappeyarkaḷ
pampāy maṇṭalattiṉ teṉpakutiyil vāḻntavar vēḷir eṉṉum  tamiḻa vakuppār eṉpatu mēṟkūṟappaṭṭatu.
vāṇiyaṉ (vāṇikaṉ) eṉṉum kuṭippeyar paṉiyā eṉṟum, ceṭṭi eṉṉum kuṭippeyar cēṭ(ṭu) eṉṟum, vaṭanāṭṭil vaḻaṅku kiṉṟaṉa. eṭṭu-eṭṭi-ceṭṭi. eṭṭutal= uyartal. eṭṭam=uyaram. eṭṭi=uyarntōṉ. cirēṣṭa eṉpatu cirēyas eṉpataṉ uccattaram (Superlative Degree).
pāṇṭava kavuravariṉ muṉṉōr tiṅkaḷ marapiṉarātaliṉ pāṇṭi yar vaḻiyiṉarum, tacarataṉiṉ muṉṉōr katiravaṉ marapi ṉarātaliṉ cōḻaṉ vaḻiyiṉarumāvar, muccuṭariyum mutalākak koṇṭa mūvaraca marapukaḷum toṉṟutoṭṭut teṉṉāṭ ṭilēyē iruntu vanta miyum, muṉpu pāṇṭiyarum piṉpu cēracōḻarum vaṭanāṭṭiyum tuṇiyaracarikkoṇṭu āṇṭamiyum, cipi vaṭanāṭṭuk katirava marapiṟkum teṉṉāṭṭuk katirava marapiṟkum potu muṉṉōṉākak kūṟappaṭutalum, ataṉāl cōḻaṉ cempiyaṉ eṉṟu peyar peṟṟamiyum nōkkuka.
3. centamiḻum koṭuntamiḻum
tamiḻar vaṭakkē cellaccella. taṭpaveppanili māṟṟam, cōmpal, pulavariṉmi, tamiḻp puravalariṉmi, putiya cuṟṟuccārpu, tāynāṭṭoṭu toṭarpiṉmi mutaliya karaṇiyaṅkaḷāl, tamiḻ tirintu koṭuntamiḻ eṉappaṭṭatu. tiriyāta teṉṉilat tamiḻ centamiḻ eṉap peṟṟatu. cilar koṭuntamiḻ tiruntic centamiḻāyiṟ ṟeṉpar. avar aṟiyār. tamiḻ vaḷarcciyil muṟpaṭṭa tiruntāta nilikaḷellām kumarikkaṇṭattiṉ teṉpākattilēyē tīrntu viṭṭaṉa.
koṭuntamiḻc coṟkaḷ potuvāyp, poruḷ tirinta col, vaṭivutirinta col, olitirinta col, centamiḻil vaḻak kaṟṟa col, putuccol eṉa aivakippaṭum.
e-ṭu:
	tamiḻ	teluṅku

iṉi, iṟṟit tamiḻil vaḻakkaṟṟut teluṅkiṟ putuccoṟ pōl tōṉṟuvaṉavaṟṟiṟ kellām vērccol kumarināṭṭil vaḻaṅkiṉa eṉṟu koḷḷavum iṭamuṇṭu.
talikkaḻaka aḻivum iṭiyīṭum
tōrā. ki. mu. 4500 pōl, pāṇṭiyaṉ kaṭuṅkōṉ kālattil, arapikkaṭa luḷḷaviṭattu nilappakutiyum, nāvalan tīviṉ teṉ pakutiyāṉa kumarikkaṇṭamum kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiṉa. vaṅkāḷak kuṭāk kaṭaliṉum arapikkaṭal muntiyatākum. ataṉālēyē, kaṭal teyvamākiya vāraṇaṉi mēṟṟicit talivaṉ eṉṟum, vaṅkāḷak kuṭākkaṭalit toṭukaṭal eṉṟum kūṟiṉar.
kuṇāatu kariporu toṭukaṭaṟ kuṇakkum             (puṟam.6)
talikkaḻakattip puranta pāṇṭiyar eṇpattoṉpatiṉmar 
eṉṟum, ak kaḻakattiṉ iṟutikkālap pulavar ainnūṟṟu nāṟpat toṉpatiṉmar eṉṟum iṟiyaṉār akapporuḷuri kūṟum.
peru(mutu)nāri, peruṅ(mutu)kuruku, kaḷariyāviri mutaliya nūlkaḷ talikkaḻakattāl iyaṟṟappeṟṟaṉavākac collap peṟum.
4. iṭikkaḻakam (tōrā. ki. mu. 4000-1500)
talikkaḻakam aḻintu ēṟattāḻa 500 āṇṭukaṭkuppiṉ, kumariyāṟu kaṭaloṭu kalanta iṭattil, alivāy eṉṟō kayavāy eṉṟō, katavam eṉṟō putavam eṉṟō peyar peṟ ṟirunta tuṟi nakaril, veṇṭērcceḻiyaṉ eṉṉum pāṇṭiyaṉ iṭikkaḻakatti niṟuviṉāṉ. aṉṟum āriyarumilli; āriyak kalappuḷḷa nūlu milli.
iṭikkaḻaka irukkiyik kapāṭapuram eṉṟu vaṭamoḻi yilakkiyam kūṟum. maturiyi maturāpuri eṉṟu virittalpōl, kapāṭattiyum kapāṭapuram eṉṟu virittirukkalām. katavam eṉpatu peyarāyiṉ, kapāṭa eṉpatu ilakkaṇappōlit tiripām; alivāy allatu kayavāy eṉpatu peyarāyiṉ, kapāṭa eṉpatu arippeyar moḻipeyarppām; putavam eṉpatu peyarāyiṉ, atu muḻuppeyar moḻipeyarppām. kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam pukār (āṟu kaṭaliṟ pukumiṭam) eṉṟu peyarpeṟṟiruntamiyum, keṭilam kaṭaloṭu kūṭumiṭam kūṭalūr eṉṟu peyar peṟṟuḷḷa miyum nōkkuka.
5. koṭuntamiḻum tiraviṭamum
orukālattil koṭuntamiḻ eṉṟirunta ticimoḻikaḷ (Regional Dialects), piṟkālattil tiraviṭam eṉṉum kiḷimoḻi kaḷāyt tirintuviṭṭaṉa. tamiḻar aṟiya, mutalāvatu tirinta kiḷi moḻi teluṅkē. atu tirinta kālam ēṟattāḻak ki.mu. 1500. teluṅku nāṭṭiṟkut teṟkē nīṇṭa kālamāyt tamiḻoṉṟē vaḻaṅki vantatu.
vaṭavēṅkaṭam teṉkumari
 āyiṭit
 tamiḻkūṟu nallulakattu
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kālap paṉampāraṉār kūṟiyatu pōṉṟē,
neṭiyōṉ kuṉṟamun toṭiyōḷ puvamun
 tamiḻvaram paṟutta taṇpuṉal naṉṉāṭṭu	(cilap. 8:1,2)
eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum, kaṭikkaḻakak kālamvari vēṅkaṭat tiṟkut teṟkil tamiḻtavira vēṟemmoḻiyum vaḻaṅkātirun tamiyik kuṟittal kāṇka.
iṉi, tiraviṭamum, (1) vaṭatiraviṭam, (2) naṭuttiraviṭam,              (3) teṉtiraviṭam eṉa muttiṟappaṭum. ivaṟṟi muṉtiraviṭam, iṭittiraviṭam, piṉtiraviṭam eṉṟum aḻikkalām.
mutaṟkālattil neṭilkaḷē tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiṉa. piṉpu avaṟṟiṉ kuṟilkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ pintit tōṉ ṟiya neṭilkaḷātaliṉ, avaṟṟiṉ kuṟilkaḷum pintiyē tōṉṟi yuḷḷaṉa. ekara okarak kuṟilvarikaṭku mikikkuṟi yiruppatē itaṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟām. ivviru kuṟilkaḷum tōṉṟu muṉṉarē, orucārt tamiḻar vintiyamaliyaṭuttum ataṟkappālum kuṭiyēṟi yiruntirukkiṉṟaṉar. avar moḻiyē piṉpu cūracēṉi, mākati, makārāṭṭiram mutaliya pirākirutaṅkaḷākap pirintupōyiruk kiṉṟatu. ap pirākirutaṅkaḷiṉ tiripē inti, vaṅkam, marātti, kucarātti mutaliya iṟṟi moḻikaḷ. ivaṟṟil ekara okarak kuṟilkaḷ illi. āyiṉ, ivaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭic coṟkaḷ tamiḻā yiruppatuṭaṉ, toṭaramippilum ivi tamiḻiyē muṟṟum ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiluḷḷa eṇkaḷum orumi paṉmi yeṉṉum iraṇṭē. intiyil āṇ peṇ eṉṉum iru pōlē uḷa. im moḻikaḷil vaḻaṅkum maraputtoṭar kaḷiyum paḻamoḻikaḷiyum nōkkiṉ, ivaṟṟip pēcum makkaḷiṉ muṉṉōr tiraviṭarā yiruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṉum muṭipiṟkē varamuṭiyum.
marāttiyum kucarāttiyum orukālattil tiraviṭa moḻi kaḷāyk koḷḷappeṟṟu, pañcatiraviṭattiṉ iru kūṟukaḷāyk kuṟikkap paṭṭaṉa. marāttiyi aṭuttut teṟkē vaḻaṅkuvatu teluṅku.
vintiyamalikku vaṭakkiluḷḷa inti, vaṅkam mutaliya moḻikaḷi vaṭatiraviṭam allatu muṉtiraviṭam eṉṟum, am maliyi yaṭutta marātti, kucarātti mutaliya moḻikaḷi naṭut tiraviṭam allatu iṭittiraviṭam eṉṟum, avaṟṟiṟkut teṟ kiluḷḷa teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam mutaliya moḻikaḷit teṉ tira viṭam allatu piṉtiraviṭam eṉṟum koḷḷiṉum poruntum.
iṟṟi niliyil inti vaṅka mutaliyavaṟṟi vaṭanāvalam eṉṟum, marātti  kucarāttiyi naṭunāvalam eṉṟum, koḷvatum poruttamām.
tamiḻ vaṭakkē ceṉṟu tiraviṭamāyt tirintateṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟāka, mutaṟkaṇ teṉtiraviṭattil teṉkōṭi moḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa teluṅkiṉiṉṟum vaṭakōṭi moḻikaḷuḷ  oṉṟāṉa pirākuvīyiṉiṉṟum, cila coṟkaḷ īṇṭu eṭuttukkāṭṭap peṟum.

7. pirākuvīt tirivu
tamiḻ vēṅkaṭattiṟku vaṭakkē ceṉṟu tiraviṭamāyt tirintatuṭaṉ, tiraviṭamoḻikaḷum vaṭakkē cellaccella mēṉ mēlum tirintum, ciṟuttum, citintum, citaṟiyum, ilakkiyamaṟṟum, āriyamāka māṟiyum pōyiṉa.
vaṭamēṟkōṭit tiraviṭa moḻiyāṉa pirākuvīc coṟkaḷ varumāṟu:

cila pirākuvīt toṭariyaṅkaḷ
ī arēṭ = nāṉ irukkiṟēṉ.
nī kāca  = nī pōkiṟāy.
nāpiṉ tēr ē? = uṉ peyar eṉṉa?
kaṉā pāva hamē cuṉ urāṭṭī tūlik = eṉ appaṉār antac ciṉṉa uṟiyil (vīṭṭil) kuṭiyirukkiṟār.
kh, gh mutaliya cila mūccoli yeḻuttukaḷum, cār (char), tantam  mutaliya inti vaṭamoḻiccoṟkaḷum, pirākuvīyiṟ kalantu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
tamiḻiṉ kumarikkaṇṭat tōṟṟatti yaṟiyātum, tamiḻiṉ iyalpiyum tiraviṭattiṉ tiripiyum nōkkātum, perumpāl mēli moḻinūlaṟiñar, anākarika nili nākarika nilikku muntiya teṉṉum neṟimuṟiyik kuruṭṭuttaṉamāyp piṉpaṟṟi, tiraviṭam tiruntit tamiḻāṉateṉṟum, pirākuvī muntunili moḻiyeṉ ṟum, tamiḻar vaṭamēṟkiṉiṉṟu peluccittāṉa vaḻiyāyt teṟku vantā reṉṟum, vantavaḻiyil peluccittāṉam iruppatāl aṅkattu moḻi anākarika niliyil uḷḷateṉṟum, teṟku vantapiṉ tam moḻiyi vaḷarttukkoṇṭā reṉṟum, uṇmikku muṟṟum māṟākak kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.
oru moḻiyiṉ coṟkaḷ, muntunili, citinili ākiya iru niliyilum kuṟuvaṭivu koṇṭu niṟkum. pirākuvīccoṟkaḷ kuṟu vaṭivukoṇṭiruppatu, citiniliyēyaṉṟi muntunili yaṉṟu. vāykaḷ eṉṉum oru colliṉ citivi nōkkiṉum iv vuṇmi viḷaṅkiviṭum.
vaḻi-(vayi)-vāy=uṇavu pukum vaḻi. kaḷḷutal kalattal allatu kūṭutal. paṉmi oru poruḷiṉ kūṭṭamātalāl, kaḷ eṉṉum col paṉmiyīṟāyiṟṟu.
vāy-pā (pi.) fŸ-¡ (ã.), vāykaḷ-pāk (pi.) iṅṅaṉamē piṟavum.
8. ticimoḻit terippu (Regional Dialectic Selection)
tamiḻil mikunta colvaḷa muṇṭu. oru celvattāy vīṭṭiṉiṉṟu, oruvakippaṭṭa pala poruḷkaḷuḷ ovvoṉṟi ovvoru putalviyum eṭuttuc celvatupōl, tamiḻiluḷḷa oru poruṭ pala coṟkaḷuḷ ovvoṉṟi ovvoru ticimoḻi yum kiyāṇṭuḷḷatu.
v-L: ïš (bj.), kid (f.), vīṭu (ma.).
9. kuṭiyēṟṟap pātukāppu (Colonial Preservation)
āyirakkaṇakkāṉa ulakavaḻakkuc coṟkaḷik koṇṭa kumarikkaṇṭam muḻukip pōṉamiyālum, pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa kuṟiyīṭukaḷiyum ceyyuṭ coṟkaḷiyum koṇṭa mutaliru kaḻaka vilakkiyam muṟṟum aḻikkappaṭṭuviṭṭamiyālum, iṉṟu orucila paḻañcoṟkaḷiṉ muntuvaṭivic cila ticimoḻikaḷē tāṅki niṟkiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu:	cey-ki.	chey (bj.)=if
	mš-m‹W	mšÈ (f.)=m§F
	aritu-ātu (etirmaṟi yiṭiniliyum īṟum). arutu(ma)

	nīṉ-niṉ, nīm, nim, nīṅkaḷ-niṅkaḷ (vēṟṟumiyaṭi)

tamiḻiṟ ceyyā eṉṉum vāypāṭṭuāvīṟṟu viṉic col, iṟantakāla viṉiyeccamākum. intiyil iv vāypāṭṭuc col iṟantakāla muṟṟum eccamumākum.

mutaṉili	i.kā. muṟṟum eccamum
	ā = vā                  		āyā = vantāṉ, vantu.
	pōl = col        	pōlā = coṉṉāṉ, colli.
ceytu eṉṉum vāypāṭṭuc col, tamiḻil oru kālattil muṟṟākavum iruntatupōṉṟē, ceyyā eṉṉum vāypāṭum iruntirukkalām.
jāttā hi, pōlttā hi mutaliya nikaḻkāla inti viṉi muṟṟukaḷ, pōttāṭu, cepputtāṭu mutaliya teluṅku nikaḻ kāla viṉimuṟṟukaḷi orumaruṅku ottamintiruntatal kāṇka.
muṉṉili viṉi
iya eṉṉum tamiḻ viyaṅkōḷīṟu, intiyil iyē eṉṉum matippu ēvalīṟākat tirintuḷḷatu.
e-ṭu:  pōliyē=colluṅkaḷ, colluka.

tamiḻil al īṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar viyaṅkōḷ eṉṉum ēval vakiyākavum payaṉpaṭuvatupōl, intiyil ṉā' īṟṟut toḻiṟ peyar ēvalākavum āḷappeṟukiṉṟatu.

iṉi, karṉā allatu ataṉ tiripāṉa karṉē' vāypāṭu etirkāla viṉiyeccamākavum (infinitive mood) intiyil vaḻaṅkuvatu, ceyyavēṇṭum eṉṉum poruḷil ceyyal vēṇṭum allatu ceyalvēṇṭum eṉpatu tamiḻil vaḻaṅkuvati orupuṭi ottatē.

12. vaṭatiraviṭa moḻikaḷ āriyamāy māṟiyami
1906ām āṇṭu intiya moḻiyāyvuk kaṇakku (Linguistic Survey of  India) eṭutta kiriyarcaṉ turimakaṉār, arittiraviṭa eilbkhÊfŸ(Semi Dravidian Dialects) eṉṉum talippiṉkīḻp piṉvarumāṟu varintiruppatu, tiraviṭarukkum tamiḻarukkum ōr eccarikkiyāyuḷḷatu.
vaṭa intiyāviluḷḷa pala tiraviṭa marapiṉar tattam iṉa moḻiyi viṭṭuviṭṭu ētēṉumōr āriya naṭi moḻiyip pēcivarukiṉṟaṉar eṉṉum uṇmi, ēṟke ṉavē eṭutturikakkappaṭṭatu. halapī eṉappaṭum moḻi itaṟkoru nalla eṭuttukkāṭṭu. atu ivvāyvuk kaṇakkil marāttiyoṭu cērttuk kūṟappaṭum atu marāttiyiṉālum cattīcakaṭiyiṉālum mikutiyum taḻuvap paṭṭa kalavi moḻi.
iṅku atupōṉṟa naṭimoḻikaḷuḷ iraṇṭit tiraviṭak kuṭumpattiṟkup piṟcērkkiyākak kuṟippōm. itaṉāl, attakiya moḻikaḷil āriyattiṉ tākkuṟivu ettuṇi niṟivākavuḷḷa teṉpati, māṇavaṉ aṟintu koḷḷa muṭiyum. av viru naṭimoḻikaḷum, amaravōt tiyic cērnta latāṭi allatu rattāṭi eṉappaṭuvatum, naraciṅkapurattilum cintuvārattilum pēcap paṭum pariyā eṉappaṭuvatum ākum. ivvāyvuk kaṇakkiṟkākat tokuk kappeṟṟa kuṟippiṉpaṭi, avaṟṟip pēcuvōr toki varumāṟu:

      yjho(Ladhadi)	2,122
      pariyā (Bharia)	330
           mottam	2,452
iv viru naṭimoḻikaḷum muṉpu kōṇṭiyōṭu (Gondi) cērkkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. eṉiṉum, iṉṟu avi muṟṟum āriya vaṇṇamāyviṭṭaṉa.

17. mēliyāriyam
vaṭa tiraviṭattip pēciya vaṭanāvala makkaḷ, vaṭamēṟkākac ceṉṟu mēliyāciyāviṟ cumēriya nākarikttip parappiyum naṇ
ṇilak kaṭaṟkari nāṭukaḷil vāḻntu āriya nākarikattiṟku aṭi kōliyum, palvēṟu nāṭṭiṉaṅkaḷāyp pirintupōyiṉar eṉpatu, mokeñcōtarō-arappā nākarikattiṟkum cumēriya nākarikat tiṟku muḷḷa oṟṟumiyālum, naṇṇilak kaṭaṟkarināṭṭu makkaḷiṉ paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaṭkum tamiḻar paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaṭku muḷḷa oppumi yālum, mēliyāciya moḻikaḷilum vaṭāppi rikka moḻikaḷilum airōppiya moḻikaḷilum vaḻaṅkum tamiḻccoṟkaḷālum, aṟiyappaṭum.
vaṭamēli yāppirikkāvic cērnta kāṉāmoḻiyilum, vā,  pō, tūkku mutaliya tamiḻccoṟkaḷ aṭippaṭic coṟ kaḷāy amintiruppatu. mikak kavaṉikkattakkatām.
mokeñcōtarō muttiriyeḻuttukaḷ tamiḻeḻuttuk kaḷiṉ mūlavaṭivik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉaveṉiṉ,atu avi tamiḻe ḻuttu kaḷ vaḷarcciyaṭiyāta niliyil vaṭakkē ceṉṟa tamiḻar kiyāṇṭa eḻuttumuṟi eṉpati yallatu, tamiḻar vaṭakkiṉiṉṟu teṟkē vantār eṉṟu uṇarttātu. mēṉāṭukaḷiṟ ceyyappeṟum putuppuṉivukaḷiṉ (Inventions) paḻiya amippukaḷ intiyāvil vaḻaṅkivaruvatum, atu intiyarē avaṟṟik kaṇṭupiṭittār eṉṟu kāṭṭāmiyum, kāṇka.
āriyak kuṭumpattic cērnta airōppiya moḻikaḷuḷ, vaṭamēṟ kōṭiyil vaḻaṅkivarum tiyūttāṉiya (Teutonic) moḻikaḷ ciṟappāka āṅkila cākacaṉiyam (Anglo-Saxon), tamiḻukku mika neruṅki yirukkiṉ ṟaṉa. an nerukkattik kāṭṭum cāṉṟukaḷ varumāṟu:
(1) 	viḻuttam perumpālum mutalacivil viḻutal.

(3) 	irumiyeṇ iṉmi.

(5) 	tiyyotta muṉṉili yorumiyīṟu paṇṭiyāṅkila 		vaḻakkiliruntami.
	e-ṭu; nī ÆU¤â-Thou art

15. coṉmāṟṟat tolivukkaṇippu (Glotto-telemetry)
āriyattiṟkum tamiḻukkum (tiraviṭattiṟkum) cila coṟkaḷ tām potuvāyirukkiṉṟaṉa veṉṟum, av viru kuṭumpamoḻi kaṭkum vēṟu yātoru toṭarpumilli yeṉṟum. palar karutu kiṉṟaṉar. pala tiraviṭaccoṟkaḷ āriyattāṟ kaṭaṉmaṭṭum koḷ ḷappaṭṭaṉavēyaṉṟi, tiraviṭak kūṟukaḷ āriya moḻiyamip piṟkuṭ pukavilliyeṉṟu, pērā. parō tam camaṟkiruta moḻi' (The Sanskrit Language) eṉṉum nūliṟ kūṟiyuḷḷār. itu teṉmoḻi vaṭamoḻit toṭarpit talikīḻāka vittu āyntataṉ viḷivākum.
oru perun tāymoḻi iyaṟkiyākap paravumpōtu, āyiraṅkal toliviṟkorumuṟi ataṉ paḻañcoṟ ṟokutiyil ēṟattāḻa mūṉṟiloru paṅki iḻakkiṉṟatu. atōṭu putuccoṟkaḷum putiya collamitiyilakkaṇa vamitika ḷum pukukiṉṟaṉa. iv vuṇmiyi aṭippaṭiyāka vittu, oru moḻi taṉ mūlamoḻiyiṉiṉṟu evvaḷavu tolivu vilakiyuḷḷa teṉṟō, ettuṇit tirintuḷḷateṉṟō kaṇikkaviyalum. ituvē coṉmāṟṟat tolivuk kaṇippām.
airōppiya āriyamoḻikaḷ tamiḻiṉiṉṟu pēraḷavu tirintuḷḷa taṟku avi vilakic ceṉṟuḷḷa cēymi aḷavē karaṇiyamām. āyiṉum, iṉṟum avaṟṟiṉ muṉṉoṭṭukaḷum piṉṉoṭṭukaḷum perumpālum tamiḻc coṟkaḷākavē yuḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu: F«-F«kš-L. cmumulus, F«-L . cum.
ik kum eṉṉum collē, com, con, col, cor, co eṉṟu āṅkilattilum, sym, syn, syl eṉṟu kirēkkattilum, sam eṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyilum tirintu āyirakkaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷit tōṟṟu vittuḷḷatu. tamiḻkkūṟu āriyac collamippiṟ kalantuḷḷa teṉpataṟku, iḵtoṉṟē pōtiya cāṉṟām.
tamiḻit talikīḻāy vittāynta kālṭuvelārum, āriyat tiṟkum tiraviṭattiṟkumuḷḷa toṭarpi allatu oppumiyi piṉvarumāṟu kuṟittuḷḷār :
(1) 	kirēkkamoḻiyilum tiraviṭattilum ṉakaram uṭampaṭu meyyāka varukiṉṟatu.
(2) 	īriṉa moḻikaḷilum paṭarkkip pakarap peyarkaḷum viṉikaḷum pālīṟēṟkiṉṟaṉa.
(3) 	paṭarkki yorumic cuṭṭuppeyar allatu oṉṟaṉ pāṟpeyar, īriṉattilum jfuåW(d or t) koṇṭuḷḷatu.
(4) 	ilattīṉiṟ pōṉṟu tamiḻiṟ palaviṉpā līṟu akaramākum.
(5) 	īriṉattilum akaram cēymiyiyum ikaram aṇmi yiyum cuṭṭum.
(6) 	pāracīkattilum tiraviṭattilum takara iṭinili iṟanta kālaṅ kāṭṭum.
(7)	mutaṉilimey iraṭṭittu iṟantakālaṅ kāṭṭuvatu īriṉattilu muṇṭu.
(8) 	īriṉattilum pala viṉikaḷ mutaṉili nīṇṭu toḻiṟ peyarākiṉṟaṉa.
iṟṟit tiraviṭamoḻikaḷ
tamiḻiṉiṉṟu tirintuḷḷa tiraviṭamoḻikaḷ, piṉvarumāṟu paṉṉiraṇṭeṉac ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṟ kuṟittār kālṭuvel.

in nūṟṟāṇṭil, pērā. parōvum pērā. emaṉōvum iṟṟit tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ maliyāḷam, kōttam, tuṭavam, kuṭa kam, tuḷuvam, kaṉṉaṭam, teluṅku, kōlāmi, nāykkī. parici (parji), kaṭapam, kōṇṭī. koṇṭā, kūy, kuvī, kurukku, mālattō, pirākuvī eṉap patiṉeṭṭākak kaṇakkiṭṭuḷḷaṉar.
tiraviṭamoḻikaḷ, potuvāka, vaṭakkē cellaccellat tirin tum citintum kuṉṟiyum, teṟkē varavarat tiruntiyum virintum ilakkiyaṅkoṇṭum irukkum.
maliyāḷam teṉṉāṭṭumoḻiyum paḻañ cēranāṭṭut tamiḻiṉ tiripumāyiṉum, tuñcattu eḻuttaccaṉiṉ aṭimit taṉattāl, piṟa tiraviṭamoḻikaḷiṉum mikutiyākavum aḷaviṟantum āriya vaṇṇamākkappaṭṭatu.
3
citinilip paṭalam
(tōrā. ki. mu. 1500-iṉṟuvari)
1. iṭikkaḻaka aḻivum iṭinilikkālamum (tōrā. ki.mu. 1500-600)
cillāyiram āṇṭukaḷiruntapiṉ, iṭikkaḻaka irukki yākiya katavapuramum (?) kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiyatu.
iṭikkaḻakattip puranta pāṇṭiyarum, ak kaḻakattiṟutiyi lirunta pulavarum aimpattoṉpatiṉmar eṉa iṟiyaṉār akap poruḷuri kūṟum. icinuṇukkam, kali, viyāḻamāli yakaval, veṇṭāḷi mutaliya nūlkaḷ iṭikkaḻakattāl iyaṟṟappeṟṟaṉa vākac collappeṟum.
iṭikkaḻakattirunta nūlkaḷ eṇṇāyiratteccameṉṟu, oru cevimarapuc ceyti vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟatu.
2. vēta āriyar nāvalam (intiyā) varuki (tōrā. ki.mu. 2000 - 1500)
vaṭanāvalattiṉiṉṟu paṭippaṭiyāka vaṭamēṟkiṟ ceṉṟu, mēliyāciyā vaḻiyākavum vaṭāppirikkā vaḻiyākavum airōppā viṟkuṭ pukuntu, kāṇṭiṉēviyatti muṭṭit tirumpik kirēkkanāṭu vari paravi āriyarāka māṟiṉa iṉattiṉ oru kiḷi, kirēkkattiṟkum paḻampāracīkattiṟkum iṭippaṭṭa oru moḻiyip pēcikkoṇṭu mulli nākarika niliyil, iṭik kaḻaka aḻiviyaṭuttu intiyāviṟ kuṭ pukuntatu. akkiḷiyiṉarkku aṉṟu eḻuttumilli; ilakkiyamu milli. avar palvēṟu ciṟuteyva vaḻipāṭṭiṉarāyum vēḷvimatat tiṉarāyum pallūṉuṇ ṇikaḷāyum iruntaṉar.
3. vētakkālam (tōrā. 1500-1000)
intiya āriyar cintuveḷiyiṟ paraviyapiṉ, palvēṟu ciṟu teyva vaḻuttut tiraṭṭākiya irukkuvētam iyaṟṟappeṟṟatu. ekara okarak kuṟil illātatum teṉcol viraviyatumāṉa vētamoḻi, vēta āriya riṉ ciṟupāṉmiyiyum, muṉṉōr moḻi maṟappiyum, pirākirutam eṉṉum vaṭatiraviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ celvākkiyum, oruṅkē uṇarttum.
4. vētat tamiḻccoṟkaḷ
